This walkthrough was written for an older version of Heart & Soul and has not been updated. Encounter tables, trainer teams and event details in it may no longer match the current game. Everything else on this site is generated from the current build - trust those pages over this one where they disagree.

Editor's Note: I'd like to give a huge shoutout to Lil Dill for his vision which led to the creation of Heart & Soul, and to all the devs and playtesters who helped this vision come to fruition. Gen 2 has always been my favorite, and, in my opinion, the experience provided by Heart & Soul is second to none. It's been an absolute joy having a small role on this team. I'd also like to give a shoutout to DaniRainbow and Liquid Justice-- chances are, I'd have never been able to finish this project without the encouragement and guidance you both have constantly provided. Finally, I'd like to thank Excelsior and InfiniteBacon42 for peeking into the code and providing maps which helped ensure my information was accurate. This will be an evolving document, I'm looking forward to any feedback which helps me further polish this resource. I hope all that use it find it to be of help! Have fun! --headlocker03

Note: There are currently two sections you see with an * next to their name in the Table of Contents. This means that the section is incomplete, and will be finished at a later date. I anticipate that I will finish these relatively soon once I get back to actively playing the game.

Oak's Welcome/Settings

Welcome to Pokémon Heart and Soul! Upon starting a new game, you'll meet Professor Oak who will help get your adventure started.

Your first choice is whether your character is a boy or a girl. In H&S, the female character is modeled after Kris from Pokémon Crystal, who was the first female playable character ever!

Next, you'll be brought to a number a screens in which you can tailor various settings of the game to your liking. There are a multitude of ways to customize your experience so take your time going through all these menus. Once the game starts, you cannot change any of these settings, so be certain of what you choose! Note: For the sake of this walkthrough, I will be playing with the default settings.

After picking your settings, you'll name your character and your adventure will officially begin! Note: Throughout this walkthrough I will be using the directions of a compass to guide you around. As a reminder, North=Up, South=Down, West=Left, and East=Right

New Bark Town

Chinchou — 40% (Lv 40)
Tentacruel — 4% (Lv 40)
Lanturn — 1% (Lv 40)

You'll wake up in your bedroom. In the northwest corner of your room is a PC. Press A to interact with it and click "Item Storage", 'Withdraw Item", and then select the Potion. You have acquired your first item of the game! Potions heal 20 HP to your Pokémon, you'll be using a lot of these throughout your journey. Press "Start", "Bag", and then you can navigate through different pockets of your bag with the left/right arrows. You'll see the Potion you just got, as well as an item called EXP Share. When turned on, the exp share grants 2/3 the exp gained to the active Pokémon in battle, and then 1/4 of the exp to every party member. Turning it on makes it easier to keep all party members growing consistently. Next, walk towards the staircase in the northeast corner. As you get close, you'll be asked to set the time. Note: You can change the time after the fact, we will walk through that process once we reach our first Pokémon Center.

Afterwards, you'll walk downstairs and speak to your mother, who will tell you that Professor Elm is looking for you. She will also give you your Pokegear and Running Shoes. The most important function of your Pokegear is the Map, you can use that to see where you are throughout the world. You can make calls, but it is very important to pay attention whenever you receive calls-- they will tell you your next step at different junctures in the game.

Our next task is to meet Professor Elm who is located in his lab which is the biggest building in town. You can walk around and interact with various NPC's throughout town, including a very suspicious and aggressive character on the west side of the lab. If you try heading west out of town, an NPC will tell you it's too dangerous to leave without your own Pokémon. Let's head into the lab!

Elm's Lab- Inside you'll meet Professor Elm and he will ask you to raise a Pokémon for him. You'll get a choice between three Pokémon-- the grass type Chikorita, the fire type Cyndaquil, and the water type Totodile. After selecting your starter, you'll be given the option to nickname it. Let's take a moment to check out our new Pokémon's stats/moveset! Press Start, Pokémon, and the select your Pokémon and press Summary. The important things shown on the first screen is your Pokémon's typing, Ability, and Nature. A Pokémon's typing will be more or less effective against different types. Also, moves used by a Pokémon that share it's typing will get a 50% damage boost! A Pokémon's ability causes different effects in battle, some even have secondary effects outside of battle. Finally, a Pokémon's Nature will affect it's stat growth. For example, a Pokémon with an Adamant Nature will have a Attack stat that is boosted by 10%, while giving a 10% decrease to it's Special Attack stat. There are some natures which have a neutral effect on stats. At a later point in the game it is possible to switch Natures. The next screen will show a Pokémon's stats, held item, and the Exp Points needed to reach the next level. Assuming you don't have a neutral nature, you'll see the stat boosted by your nature highlighted in red, and the one decreased in blue. If you press R, you'll be shown your Pokémon's IVs (Internal Values). These are unchangeable values which affect your Pokémon's growth, 0 being the lowest, and 31 being the highest. Pressing L will show your Pokémon's EVs (Effort Values). Each Pokémon defeated will yield a specific amount/type of EVs, every 4 EVs equals an extra stat gain by the time your Pokémon reaches level 100. There are also items that can be used to raise EV's. You can get a max of 510 total EVs, and up to 252 in a single stat. 252 EVs equates to 63 stat points which is a huge boost. Finally, the next screen shows your Pokémon's moveset. Here you can see a moves typing along with the power/effects. After selecting your Pokémon, Elm will direct you to find Mr. Pokémon north of Cherrygrove city. He also tells you that you can use the machine in the top left corner of his lab to heal your Pokémon. As you leave the lab, Elm's assistant will give you a Potion. Time to leave our hometown by heading west towards Route 29!

Route 29

Upon entering your first route, you'll notice the patches of grass. Wild Pokémon can be found here. If you look around the route you'll see different Pokémon's sprites in the overworld--these can be used to know what Pokémon can be encountered on a route! There are no trainers to battle on this route, so you've only got these wild Pokémon to deal with. You don't have access to Pokeballs yet, so you can only battle wild Pokémon for now. You'll also notice the brown ledges as you walk through the route--you can hop over these, one way, while navigating. On the northeast side of the route, a Potion can be found. You'll see a building to the north--this leads to a small part of Route 46. We will head there in a moment, for now let's finish exploring Route 29. To the left of that building you'll see a chunky NPC named Tuscany of the Weekday Family. You'll find various family members throughout Johto who will all give you items if you can guess their favorite day of the week. Tuscany's is Tuesday, and he will gift you a Silk Scarf! Open your bag and read the effects granted by this item, it will be immediately useful no matter which starter you picked. A little further to the west you'll find a berry tree which gives 5 Oran Berries. Berry Trees will naturally replenish themselves, so be sure to check them whenever exploring. Before moving on to Cherrygrove City to the west, let's go back to that building to the north and check out Route 46.

For now, there isn't anything else to do except encounter wild Pokémon. Time to head towards Cherrygrove to the west of Route 29.

Upon entering Cherrygrove, and old man will offer to give you a tour of the town. The building with the red roof is the Pokémon Center, you can heal your Pokémon here for free and also access the PC where you can store items and Pokémon. The building with the blue roof is the Pokémart. Right now, you can only purchase Antidote's and Potion's. Pokémart's item lists update at different junctures, so be sure and check them out regularly to see what's available. Once you're done exploring the city, head north to Route 30.

Shortly after the entrance to the route you will see another berry tree yielding 5 Oran Berries. Inside the house, you can speak to an NPC to get a Cheri Berry. Up ahead, the path will branch into two, with the west path currently blocked by an ongoing trainer battle. To the east, you'll find Mr. Pokémon's house, be sure and grab the 5 Oran Berries before entering. Inside, Mr. Pokémon will give you the Mystery Egg for Elm to examine. You'll also be given a Pokedex by Professor Oak! Upon leaving the house you'll get a phone call from Elm asking you to return to the lab immediately. Before heading back, press start and open up your Pokedex. Along with a blurb describing the Pokémon, you can also see where it can be found in the wild, it's base stats, learnset, potential abilities, and means of evolution. It's an extremely useful tool as you progress through the game! Head back to Cherrygrove and heal up at the Pokécenter, we've got a battle awaiting us as we head east to leave the city...

Meeting Your Rival/Elm Revisited

Remember that pushy guy outside of Elm's lab? He's back, and challenges you to your first trainer battle! He will always have a level 5 starter that your starter is weak to. Wonder where he got that from? Head back to Elm's lab in New Bark Town to join a conversation with a police officer. You'll find out the Pokémon your rival has was stolen from Elm, and will be asked what his name was. After naming your rival and finishing this conversation, you'll hand the Mystery Egg over to Elm. He'll make note of your Pokedex and encourages you to take on the Pokémon Gym challenge. As you leave the lab, Elm's assistant will give you 5 Pokeballs. We can now start catching Pokémon! Stop by your house and let your mom know you are leaving, and then we will head back to Route 30 and make our way to Violet City, the location of the first Pokémon Gym!

Route 30 Revisited

There are a couple of things to check out before Route 30. First, the old man right outside of New Bark Town now offers an explanation on catching Pokémon. This is completely optional. Next, be sure and stop by the Pokémart in Cherrygrove, it's menu has been updated, most notably Pokeballs are now purchasable. Note: every 10 Pokeballs purchased yields a free Premier Ball. After you finish up, head north to Route 30 and take the left path that was previously blocked by a trainer battle. Now we get a turn! Your first battle will be with Youngster Joey who has a Lv 4 Rattata. Next up will be Youngster Mikey who has a Lv 2 Hoothoot and a Lv 4 Sentret. Continuing north, you'll encounter your final trainer on the route Bug Catcher Don. He has a Lv 3 Ledyba and a Lv 3 Spinarak. Continuing north will lead you to Route 31.

You'll notice a cave opening towards the beginning of the route, we will address that once we clear out Route 31. The only trainer on the route is Bug Catcher Wade, he has a Lv 4 Weedle and a Lv 5 Pineco. You'll find a berry tree which yields 5 Cheri Berries, and an Antidote in the grass. To the west is Violet City, but let's go back to that cave real quick.

Dark Cave (South)

You'll notice the cave living up to it's name right as you enter. Exploration becomes much easier once we acquire the Flash HM in Violet City. Unlike outside, wild Pokémon can appear at any time in a cave, so be wary of that fact. In the northwest corner there is a Potion on the ground. That's as far as we can explore here for now so head out and west towards Violet City.

Welcome to Violet City! Right as you enter town you'll see the Pokécenter. In the house to the left of the Pokécenter there is an NPC who will trade an Onix holding a Persim Berry for a Bellsprout. A bit further into the town and to the left of the gym, you'll see an NPC with glasses. If you answer his question with "No", he'll escort you to the Trainer School, where several NPC have helpful hints. Heading south, you'll find a berry tree with 5 Cheri Berries. An NPC will prevent further exploration south for now. Finally, head to the northern part of town where you will see Sprout Tower. Before entering, grab the Pecha Berry to the right of the tower.

Sprout Tower (HM05 Flash)

Encounter List LandRock Smash/HeadbuttSurfOld RodGood RodSuper Rod
Day (2F)Rattata — 100% (Lv 3-6)
Night (2F)Rattata — 65% (Lv 3-5)

Gastly — 35% (Lv 4-6)

The path through Sprout Tower is straightforward, start by heading up the ladder and to the west where you will battle Sage Nico. He has a Lv 3 Bellsprout, a Lv 4 Hoppip, and another Lv 3 Bellsprout. Continuing to the next floor, you'll find a Paralyz Heal on the floor. You'll battle Sage Chow who has a Lv 3 Bellsprout, a Lv 3 Sunkern, and another Lv 3 Bellsprout. On the next floor, you'll find an X Defend on the ground. You'll battle Sage Edmond who has a three Lv 3 Bellsprout. Moving on to the final floor, the first trainer to the west is Sage Jin who has a Lv 6 Bellsprout. To the east is Sage Neal who has a Lv 6 Bellsprout. Up ahead in front of the pillar is Sage Troy who has a Lv 7 Hoppip and a Lv 7 Bellsprout. Past the pillar you will have a brief conversation with your rival before battling Sage Li who has a Lv 7 Oddish, a Lv 9 Bellsprout, and a Lv 8 Sunkern. You'll also be awarded HM05 (Flash) for winning this battle! Once we acquire the Violet City gym badge, you can go back and illuminate the Dark Cave for easier exploration. Grab the Escape Rope on the floor and head out of Sprout Tower. You can explore Route 36 East and the Ruins of Alph before challenging the gym, for this walkthrough, I'll break down the gym first.

Violet City Gym

Time to get our first gym badge! Your first battle will be with Bird Keeper Rod who has a Lv 7 Pidgey and a Lv 7 Natu, you'll win ₽224 in prize money. Your next battle will be with Bird Keeper Abe who has a Lv 9 Spearow, you'll win ₽288 in prize money. Finally you'll battle Leader Falkner who has a Lv 8 Pidgey and a Lv 11 Noctowl (holding a Sitrus Berry which recovers 25% of it's health). You'll be granted the Zephyrbadge which raises your Pokémon's Attack and allows you to use Flash outside of battle. You will also be given TM32 (Double Team). Note: In H&S, a Pokémon simply needs to be able to learn a move to use it in the overworld-- there is no need to teach them the move unless you plan to use it in battle. HM moves can also be forgotten naturally.

Mystery Egg

You'll get a phone call from Elm telling you to meet his assistant at the Violet City Pokécenter. Head there, and you'll be given the Mystery Egg! This will eventually hatch into Togepi, to do so, you must carry it in your party until it hatches. Once it does, you can head back to New Bark and show Elm your Togepi/Togetic/Togekiss for a free Everstone. Our next stop will be Route 36 which is west of Violet City.

Route 36 East

Houndour and Growlithe are notable new potential encounters here. You'll also find another member of the Weekday Family name Arthur, respond to his question with Thursday and you'll be gifted a Hard Stone. If you try going west, your path is blocked by a mysterious tree. We will clear that later on. Once you're finished up, head south through the gate towards the Ruins of Alph.

Ruins of Alph/Kabuto Puzzle

As you enter, you'll see a patch of grass to the west. You'll also see a building containing scientists, the top right one can resurrect fossils. Outside there are two cave entrances. The lower, larger cave is the main Ruin, there won't be any wild encounters here until be complete another step. Head to the upper cave where you will find the first sliding puzzle room, this one being Kabuto, the final result is pictured here. You'll notice a sensor on northern wall, we will address that shortly. Once you finish the puzzle, you'll fall into a hole which takes you into the main ruin, and you'll find a Dome Fossil on the ground. The main ruin will now have wild Unown. There isn't a reward for catching them all, but if you're aiming to be a completionist, there are 28 different forms of Unown. Makes for a fun looking box! Return to the scientist to revive your fossil into a Lv 5 Kabuto, add it into your party and bring it to the front so that it is your follower and return to the puzzle room. Interacting with the sensor will now open up a room where you find an Oran Berry, a Pecha Berry, and Energypowder, and a Heal Powder. You'll then drop down into another chamber with some lore spelled out in Unown characters. The message reads, "Our words shall remain here for the ages". Drop down one more time to find yourself in the main ruin and head to the exit. We are done with the Ruins for now, there are 3 more puzzles that will be accessible later in the game. Head through the east gate towards Route 32.

This route is a bit bigger than the ones we have traversed thus far. As you head south into the route, you'll be stopped by an NPC who will gift you a Miracle Seed. Continuing south, you'll battle Youngster Albert who has a Lv 7 Sentret and a Lv 7 Zubat. Below and slightly to the west, you'll find a Great Ball in the grass. Close to that will be Picnicker Liz who has a Lv 9 Nidoran (F). Continuing south, you'll notice the path splits in two, we will keep left and battle Camper Roland who has a Lv 9 Nidoran (M). Continuing south, you'll battle Youngster Gordon who has a Lv 10 Wooper. Shortly south (before going down the stairs) you'll find a Great Ball on the ground. Just north of the stairs you'll find a Potion on the ground. After descending the stairs, you'll notice a path going east and then north, we will go this way to clear out the trainers we previously skipped. First up will be Fisherman Henry who has a Lv 7 Marill and a Lv 7 Poliwag. Next will be Fisherman Ralph who has a Lv 8 Goldeen. Finally we will battle Fisherman Justin who has a Lv 7 Tentacool. Head back south to have a sketchy character try and sell you a Slowpoke Tail for ₽1,000,000, say no and heal up at the Pokécenter. Inside you'll meet a fisherman who will give you the Old Rod. Exit the Pokécenter face the final trainer on the route Bird Keeper Peter who has a Lv 8 Spearow and a Lv 8 Natu. Heal up if you need to at the Pokécenter and let's head into Union Cave!

Welcome to Union Cave! We'll be coming back through later to reach spots that are currently inaccessible. To start we will battle Hiker Russell who has a Lv 9 Geodude and a Lv 8 Cubone. Continuing west, you'll find a Potion on the ground and, south from there, you'll find an Awakening on the ground. From there, you'll head east and battle Hiker Daniel who has a Lv 11 Onix. Next will be Firebreather Bill who has a Lv 11 Growlithe. Below him is a Great Ball on the ground. As you head east and then south, you'll notice staircase which leads to a battle with Pokemaniac Larry who has a Lv 10 Larvitar. Take the ladder down to B1F where you will find an X Defend on the ground. You can also find Pikablu Marill down here. Head back up the ladder and continue east until you see a path that goes south, you'll find an X Attack on the ground at the end of this path. Head back to the branch and head east where you will battle Firebreather Ray who has a Lv 9 Vulpix. Shortly after you will find the exit of the cave to Route 33.

This is another tiny route, as you exit Union Cave, head south and collect 5 Pecha Berries from a berry tree. Head east to battle the only trainer, Hiker Anthony, who has a Lv 11 Geodude and a Lv 11 Machop. Head east past the Slowpoke Well currently blocked by a Team Rocket grunt and we will arrive at Azalea Town.

Azalea Town

Encounter List LandRock Smash/HeadbuttSurfOld RodGood RodSuper Rod
Pineco — 60% (Lv 10)
Spinarak — 30% (Lv 10)
Aipom — 5% (Lv 10)

Azalea is a small town, the path to the west leads to Ilex Forest which we will explore later. Heal up at the Pokécenter if needed, and head towards the house in the northwest corner of town. Before heading inside, collect 5 Pecha Berries from the berry tree. Inside you'll meet the pokeball maker Kurt who will inform you that Team Rocket are cutting off Slowpoke tails in the Slowpoke Well and then rushes off to stop them. Better follow and see if he needs help.

Kurt has certainly seen better days--he'll ask us to take on Team Rocket in his stead. The first Rocket Grunt has a Lv 9 Rattata and a Lv 11 Rattata. The next Rocket Grunt has a Lv 9 Rattata and a Lv 9 Zubat. You'll find a Super Potion on the ground, and a Great Ball on the ground south from the Super Potion. Rocket Eto has a Lv 11 Elekid, a Lv 11 Magby, and a Lv 11 Smoochum. Finally we take on Rocket Admin Proton who has a Lv 12 Koffing, a Lv 15 Houndour, and a Lv 13 Slowpoke. After the battle, Kurt will congratulate you and take you back to his house. You can now give Kurt berries to turn into special type-effective pokeballs, the best you can get aside from the Master Ball and one other that will remained unnamed for the moment. The balls made by Kurt sell for ₽500 a piece, so this is a good way to farm money whenever necessary. The gym is now open, so we will head there and get our second badge.

Azalea Town Gym

Bugsy's gym has multiple platforms which will take you to different parts in the gym. If you're wanting to skip the trainers and head directly to Bugsy, you'll take the right platform and then the left. We will go ahead and battle all the trainers for the sake of this walkthrough. The first middle platform will take you to Bug Catcher Benny who has a Lv 9 Kakuna and a Lv 12 Beedrill. Back at the beginning, taking the right platform will start a double battle with Twins Amy & May who have a Lv 12 Ledyba and a Lv 12 Spinarak. Head back to the beginning and take the left platform to get to the second set of platforms. The middle platform will take you to Bug Catcher Josh who has a Lv 12 Yanma. Head back and take the left platform for a battle with Bug Catcher Al who has a Lv 9 Metapod and a Lv 12 Butterfree. The last platform for us to take is to the right which will take us to Leader Bugsy who has a Lv 15 Pineco, a Lv 16 Scyther (holding a berry juice), and a Lv 15 Ledian. Bugsy will award you the Hivebadge which makes all Pokémon up to level 30 obey you and allows you to use Cut in the overworld, as well as the ability to Headbutt trees. You'll also be given TM09 (Bullet Seed). Be sure and heal up once you leave the gym, we have a battle with our Rival shortly.

Rival Battle 2

As you try to head west out of Azalea, your Rival will show up and challenge you to a battle. Below are the 3 different partys that he will use, dependent on what starter you chose. After the battle, heal up and then head west through the gate to Ilex Forest!

Ilex Forest/HM01(Cut)

Welcome to Ilex Forest! Like caves, wild Pokémon can appear anywhere in the forest. As you follow the path, you'll meet an NPC who had a Farfetch'd take off on him. Let's help the little guy! Continuing on, you'll see the Farfetch'd in the overworld. Interact with it to cause it run off, there will be 6 times where you will interact with the Farfetch'd before it will get back to it's owner. Note: be sure and interact with it from behind the 6th time, otherwise you might accidentally redirect it back into the forest which starts the process all over again. Once you've finished, you'll be awarded HM01(Cut). We will backtrack and collect a few items now available to us due to Cut once we finish up the Ilex Forest section, feel free to skip ahead to the next section for those locations if you want to save yourself from having to walk back through the forest. Use cut on the tree nearby to continue through the forest. You'll notice the Ilex Forest Shrine, which has some fun in store for us later on. Keep left when the path branches and you'll see an Ether on the ground. Further ahead will be a Full Heal on the ground. Continue on, once again taking left at the fork, and then right at the next one, you'll find TM11 (Sunny Day) on the ground. Backtrack to the previous fork and go left to find an NPC who can teach your Pokémon Headbutt. Hop over the ledge and head back up north to find a Kimono Girl lost in the forest. No matter how you answer her question, your follower will lead her to the exit and immediately return to you. Head west to have a battle with Bug Catcher Wayne who has a Lv 15 Paras and a Lv 15 Oddish. You'll notice an NPC close to the exit that can teach your Pokémon Fury Cutter. The exit ahead will lead to Route 34, but first we will backtrack and collect a few items, so head back to Azalea.

Item Backtrack 1

First, we will stop by the Charcoal Kiln on the southeast corner of Azalea where the apprentice we previously helped will give you a Charcoal. Next you'll go through Union cave back to Route 32 where you can now cut the tree blocking the path to another member of the Weekday Family Frieda--answer her question with Friday to acquire a Poison Barb. Head north through the route to find another tree which leads to an NPC who gives you TM05 (Roar), there is also a Super Potion on the ground. Finally, head back to Violet City to find another tree just north of the Pokécenter, you'll find a Rare Candy on the ground. That's it for now so make your way back through Ilex Forest and get ready for Route 34.

Route 34

Shortly after entering the route you will battle Youngster Samuel who has a Lv 12 Teddiursa, a Lv 12 Spearow, and a Lv 12 Sandshrew. Follow the road east and then north to battle PokeFan Brandon who has a Lv 15 Snubbull. Head west into the grass to battle Camper Todd who has a Lv 15 Azumarill. Head north back to the path to battle Youngster Ian who has a Lv 12 Mankey and a Lv 14 Diglett. You'll notice a building to the east, we will come back here as soon as we finish clearing out the route. Further north you have a battle, that only takes place at night, with Officer Keith who has a Lv 16 Growlithe. You'll find a Super Potion on the ground in the grass to the east, as well as Picnicker Gina who has a Lv 14 Skiploom and a Lv 14 Bulbasaur. That's it for this route for now, so head back to that building we recently skipped.

Day Care

Welcome to the Day Care! You can leave up to 2 Pokémon here, and they will gain 1 exp for every step you take. There is a base charge of ₽100 per Pokémon, and then another ₽100 per level gained. You can breed Pokémon to produce eggs if two Pokémon of opposite genders are from the same egg group (I recommend googling what these are). Ditto can breed with anything, including genderless Pokémon. The exceptions to the rule are Legendaries, Unown, Baby Pokémon, Nidorina, and Nidoqueen. I'd recommend breeding with Ditto to simplify the process, you can find them right outside on Route 34. The daycare couple's son will offer the Odd Egg to you if respond to his question correctly. The species of the Pokémon within is dependent on your response, the answers can be found by reading the bookcases left to right, but I'll also give you all possible phrases below. Whatever Pokémon hatches from this egg has a 14% chance of being shiny (having a unique color palette). Once you're finished here, head north to Goldenrod City.

Answers 1 & 2: BABE, POKEMON

[Answer 3], [Answer 4]

Pichu: CUTE, STATIC

Cleffa: CUTE, CUTE CHARM

Igglybuff: SONG, CUTE CHARM

Tyrogue: FIGHTING, GUTS

Smoochum: ICE, OBLIVIOUS

Elekid: ELECTRIC, STATIC

Magby: FIRE, FLAME BODY

Goldenrod City

Welcome to Goldenrod City! To the north is Route 35. As you enter Goldenrod City, you'll get a phonecall from Elm reminding you to swing by his lab and show him the Pokémon which hatched from the Mystery Egg if you haven't already. Head back to New Bark Town with Togepi/Togetic/Togekiss as your follower and Elm will give you an Everstone. Back at Goldenrod, you'll notice the Goldenrod Dept Store to your right as you enter the city. You'll find a greater variety of items to purchase here than you would at a normal Pokémart. Before heading upstairs to check out the different shops, take the elevator down to Floor 0. Talking to the NPCs down here will cause the Machokes to move the boulders out of your way. You'll find an Exp Share S, an Ether, and an Ultra Ball on the floor. Head back up to 1F and go up the stairs. As you ascend, you'll notice signs explaining what is sold on each floor. On the 5th floor during the day, there will be an NPC to the left of the TM vendor who will give you one of two TMs depending on your friendship level with your following Pokémon. For those that have very high friendship (heart in summary either nearly full or gold), you'll be given TM27 (Return). For those that have minimal friendship, you'll be given TM21 (Frustration). In my experience, the latter is most easily obtained by catching a wild Pokémon, entering a battle, and then allowing it to be attacked while you recover with medicinal herbs (items that heal at the cost of friendship), and then allowing your partner to be knocked out. We'll soon meet a vendor who sells these herbs. On the 6th floor, you'll find an NPC who will trade you a Machop holding a Macho Brace for a Bellsprout. The only thing left to do here is to stock up on whatever items you'd like, and then head outside. Our next stop will be the house you see directly to the east of the Dept Store entrance, so head north, east, and then south to reach the bike shop where you'll be given the Bike. You can change the speed of your bike by pressing R while stationary. Head back to the main path, go north and then east at the Gym and enter the Flower Shop beside the gym. The NPC on the south side of the shop will gift you a random Berry. Exit the shop and head west across the main road and enter the first house to meet the Name Rater. Finaly, head outside and to the building west of the Name Rater to enter the Goldenrod Underground.

Goldenrod Underground/Game Corner

Your first battle will be with Super Nerd Eric who has a Lv 15 Grimer and a Lv 15 Cubone. You'll find a Paralyz Heal on the ground, and then battle Super Nerd Teru who has a Lv 14 Voltorb, a Lv 14 Magnemite, and a Lv 14 Porygon. Head down the hallway to the east to get the Coin Case. Continuing south you'll notice 3 tables potentially staffed by different NPCs depending on the time of day. At nighttime, the first table will be staffed by the younger Salon Brother who can give your Pokémon a friendship boosting haircut for ₽300. During the daytime, the middle table is staffed by the older Salon Brother whose service costs ₽500 and boosts friendship even more than his yonger brother. The final table will perpetually be staffed by the Herb Lady. Herbs are cheap healing items which decrease friendship upon use. You'll find an Antidote and a Super Potion on the floor before battling Pokemaniac Donald who has two Lv 15 Slowpokes. The next trainer is Pokemaniac Isaac who has a Lv 14 Lickitung. Take the exit to the south to wind back up in Goldenrod and then head north to the Game Corner where you can now buy coins which can be used to purchase Pokémon and some strong TMs. Rather than buying coins, you have the option to play the slots or roulette to get coins. Once you are finished up here, exit the Game corner and head north on the main street, and take the first path heading east to arrive at the Radio Tower.

Radio Tower

You'll notice an NPC with pink hair named Whitney at the counter as you enter the Radio Tower. Speak to the attendant behind the table and answer the questions with Yes, Yes, No, Yes, No, to receive the Radio. Whitney will say it's time for her to head back to the gym, opening the gym to challengers. Talking to the attendent on the east side of the table will grant you entry into the Goldenrod Loto--a random trainer id number will be generated and you can win different prizes if any Pokémon you own have trainer id numbers which match. The prizes are: 2 numbers matched yields a PP Up, 3 yield a Macho Brace, 4 yield a Max Revive, and a perfect match of all 5 numbers yields a Master Ball! You can do this once per day. That's it for the Tower, to the north of Goldenrod, Route 35, the National Park, and part of Route 36 are open for exploration and have multiple trainers/Pokémon to face. If you need more training before the gym, skip ahead to Route 35 and finish your preparations because we will challenge the Goldenrod City Gym next!

Goldenrod City Gym

Head north as you enter the gym to battle Lass Carrie who has a Lv 18 Happiny. Continuing up and around, you'll battle Beauty Samantha who has a Lv 18 Meowth. Next will be Beauty Victoria who has a Lv 15 Teddiursa and a Lv 17 Furret. The final gym trainer before facing Whitney will be Lass Bridget who has a Lv 16 Aipom and a Lv 16 Munchlax. Finally you'll have a battle with Leader Whitney who has a Lv 20 Clefairy, a Lv 20 Teddiursa, and the infamous Lv 21 Miltank holding a Sitrus Berry. Upon defeat, Whitney will start crying and not immediately hand over her badge, but after trying to leave and having Lass Bridget inform you this is a regular occurrence, talk to Whitney again to be given the Plainbadge which raises your Pokémon's attack and allows you to use Strength outside of battle. You'll also be given TM45 (Attract). The next thing we will do is stop by the Flower Shop next door and talk to the NPC on the west side of the building, she will give you the Squirtbottle which we can use to clear that weird tree we saw earlier on Route 36. Heal up and head north towards Route 35. Within the gate on the way out of town, speak to the guard, he will ask you to deliver a Lv 20 Spearow to his friend on Route 31. We will soon have a shortcut there, so continue on to Route 35.

Route 35

Head north to start a battle with Picnicker Kim who has a Lv 18 Vulpix. Continue on to battle Camper Elliot who has a Lv 16 Sandshrew and a Lv 16 Marill, and then Picnicker Brooke who has a Lv 18 Pikachu. Next up we'll battle Camper Ivan who has a Lv 16 Eevee and a Lv 16 Diglett, then Juggler Irwin who has two Lv 16 Voltorbs and a Lv 16 Pineco. Firebreather Walt has two Lv 15 Magmars, and to the right of the gate up ahead at night, you will face Officer Dirk who has a Lv 16 Growlithe and a Lv 16 Houndour. We won't head through the gate quite yet, instead head east into the grass to battle Bug Catcher Arnie who has a Lv 18 Yanma and a Lv 18 Venonat. Head south and then up and around a bend to find a Paralyz Heal on the ground. Head back to the grass and head south to battle Bird Keeper Bryan who will have a Lv 18 Pidgey, a Lv 18 Pidgeotto, and a Lv 18 Spearow, you'll find TM39 (Rock Tomb) on the ground a little to the east. That's it for Route 35, head back and heal up if needed and then head through the gate to the north into the National Park.

National Park

Within the gate leading to the park, you can talk to a guard (dressed in green) to take part in the Bug Catching Contest. You'll be given 30 Safari Balls (which disappear once the contest is finished) and take the first Pokémon in your party. You'll face a unique set of encounters during contests with the aim being to catch the strongest Pokémon possible (Scyther and Pinser will always yield 1st place). The rewards are:

1st Place: Either a Leaf Stone, Moon Stone, or Sun Stone

2nd Place: Either a Fire Stone, Thunder Stone, or Water Stone

3rd Place: Random Berry

Any experience earned will be reverted once the contest ends. Now let's clear out the interior without taking part in the contest. As you enter, head east to find an NPC on a bench next to a Persian, she will give you a Quick Claw. Head north into the grass to battle School Kid Jack who has a Lv 16 Sunflora and a Lv 17 Voltorb. Towards the west in that same patch of grass you'll battle Parasol Lady Beverly who has a Lv 20 Snubbull. Head north into the taller grass to battle Lass Krise who has a Lv 17 Skiploom and a Lv 16 Cubone. To the east in that same grass you'll battle Pokefan William who has a Lv 15 Raichu. Towards the northwest corner will be an old lady who will heal your party. Cut the tree just east from her and head east around the corner to find a Soothe Bell on the ground. Retrace your steps and head west and then south to eventually find TM28 (Dig). That's it for the National Park, heal up with that old lady if needed and exit through the east gate to arrive on the west side of Route 36.

Route 36 West/HM06 Rock Smash

As you enter the route, head north to grab 5 Persim Berries from the berry tree. Moving along the route will lead to a battle with Psychic Mark who has two Lv 15 Abras and a Lv 16 Kadabra, and then School Kid Alan who has a Lv 20 Tangela and a Lv 20 Growlithe. Up ahead, use the Squirtbottle on the tree to have an encounter with a Lv 20 Sudowoodo. Note: if you attempt to run away, Sudowoodo will remain on the map, interact with it again to start a new encounter. Immediately after catching or defeating Sudowoodo, you'll notice an NPC jumping to the east. Speak to him to get HM06 (Rock Smash). Note: Whenever you smash rocks with Rock Smash, there will be a chance you receive a Red Shard, Blue Shard, Yellow Shard, Green Shard, or Heart Scale. You may also encounter wild Pokémon. We now have a straight shot to Route 31, so head east through Violet City and give Kenya the Spearow to the chubby guy next to the berry tree, you'll be given TM41 (Torment). Return to the gate north of Goldenrod to get an HP Up from the guard who asked you to make the delivery. Head back and then west through the path previously blocked by Sudowoodo to end up at Route 37.

Route 37

Keep north at the fork to battle Twins Ann & Anne who have a Lv 18 Clefairy and a Lv 18 Furret. Close by will be another member of the Weekday Family name Sunny, answer his question with "Sunday" to be given a Magnet. Head back down to the fork in the path and head east and the north to battle Psychic Greg who has a Lv 22 Stantler. Up ahead you can collect 5 Chesto Berries from a berry tree. Continue north to enter Ecruteak City.

Ecruteak City

Welcome to Ecruteak City! To the east is Route 38, and to the west is Route 42. In the northwest corner, you'll find a Rare Candy on the ground. From here, head to the Pokécenter to meet Bill.

Pokécenter (Bill)/Eevee

Inside the Pokécenter you will meet Bill at the PC. He mentions you can visit him anytime in Goldenrod--let's do that now! Head back to Goldenrod, go into the alley in front of the Game Corner, and then into the first house you see. Inside, speak to Bill to receive a Lv 20 Eevee! Head back to Ecruteak and into the Dance Theater just north of Ecruteak's Pokécenter.

Dance Theater/HM03 (Surf)

Inside, you'll see a Rocket harassing a Kimono Girl on stage. Speak to the Rocket Grunt for a battle, he has a Lv 20 Koffing. As you leave, an old man will give you HM03 (Surf). Our next stop will be at the Burned Tower on the northwest edge of town.

Burned Tower/Rival Battle 3/Awakening of Beasts

Eusine will introduce himself as you enter the tower. He talks about how he heard the legendary Pokémon Suicune might be in the building... East of his location you'll find an HP Up on the ground. Heading west and then north will lead you to a battle with Burglar Richardo who has a Lv 20 Koffing and a Lv 19 Growlithe. Up ahead you'll be challenged by your Rival to another battle, his potential parties are:

After the battle, you will fall into a hole that opens up beneath you. You'll find yourself in front of the three Legendary Beasts, who will awaken and immediately flee the building. Suicune will eventually be a static encounter, but now Entei will be roaming the map (meaning it can be found in random routes around Johto), and Raikou will spawn once Entei is either knocked out or caught. Both will be Lv 40, and immediately flee the battle, even if put to sleep, so my recommendation would be to train up a speedy Pokémon that knows Mean Look when you are ready to hunt them down (Gengar is my go-to). All three will be registered as "seen" in your Pokedex, so you can look at Entei's (and then Raikou's) location as you walk between routes to see if it can be found on whatever route you are currently on. Back to the tower, you'll find an Ultra Ball on the ground. Head back the ladder west across the northern part of the building to battle Firebreather Nard who has a Lv 21 Charmeleon. Head west to find an Ether on the ground. That's it for the Burned Tower! The Ecruteak Gym can now be challenged, I'll be walking through that next, but it's worth noting that you can skip to Routes 38 & 39 and go as far as the Olivine Lighthouse if you need some extra training, there are quite a few trainers to battle at those locations. For now, heal up, and let's go get our next badge!

Ecruteak City Gym

The path through this gym is a little gimmicky, start on the green tile and step forward into the darkness, your aim will to remain on the white/grey patterned path going forward, stepping off the path will warp you to the beginning of the gym. The first gym trainer is Sage Jeffrey who has a Lv 21 Ghastly, a Lv 21 Murkrow, and a Lv 21 Haunter. Next up will be Hex Maniac Martha who has a Lv 20 Haunter and a Lv 20 Houndour. Sage Ping wil have a Lv 22 Misdreavus, followed by Hex Maniac Grace who has a Lv 20 Haunter and a Lv 20 Murkrow. Finally you'll reach the Leader Morty who has a Lv 24 Haunter, a Lv 23 Stantler, a Lv 24 Misdreavus, and a Lv 25 Gengar holding a Sitrus Berry. Upon victory you'll be awarded the Fogbadge which allows all Pokémon up to Lv 50 to obey and grants the ability to use Surf in the overworld. You'll also be given TM30 (Shadow Ball). With access to Surf, there are several items now available to us that we will be collecting once we get one more HM from the next town. You can also now head back to the Flower Shop in Goldenrod to buy mints which change the nature of your Pokémon. The next 3 gyms can be taken on in any order (the gym leaders scale accordingly), I'll be taking walking us through what I feel is the most convenient path. Heal up and head west towards Route 38! Note: As of V1.2.1, it is important that you make your way west out of Ecruteak to Route 39 before heading east to Mahogany. Failure to do so can eventually lead to a progression blocking bug. This will be fixed in the future!

Route 38

Heading west out of Ecruteak will take you to Route 38. We will take the path south to begin which leads to a battle with Beauty Valerie who has a Lv 22 Skiploom and a Lv 21 Miltank. Head west to battle Bird Keeper Toby who has two Lv 22 Doduos. Heading just a bit further west across a bridge eads to a Lax Incense on the ground. Head back to where Toby is and then go north to battle Beauty Olivia who has a Lv 21 Corsola, east from here you'll see Lass Dana who has a Lv 19 Flaafy and a Lv 20 Psyduck. Head east one final time to battle Sailor Harry who has a Lv 23 Quagsire. Towards the center of the route is a berry tree which yields 5 Rawst Berries. Head west across the northern bridge to battle School Kid Chad who has a Lv 22 Mr. Mime and a Lv 22 Magnemite. Follow the path south and then west to end up on Route 39 where we will have a big decision to make as we enter the route.

Meeting Baoba/Choosing Your Legendary

As you walk into Route 39, you'll see a large shadow of a Pokémon cross the ground. An NPC will ask if you saw what color the Pokémon was. Your answer will determine which Legendary Pokémon you face before the Elite 4 (you'll get the other one later in the game). Choose "Gold" if you'd like Ho-oh or "Silver" if you'd like Lugia. For this walkthrough, I will be selecting Gold. The NPC will introduce himself as Baoba, and will tell you about the Safari Zone he plans on opening in Cianwood. You'll register each other's numbers in the Pokegear, and he'll tell you he will give you a call once the Safari Zone is open.

Route 39

Continuing west will lead to a battle with Psychic Norman who has a Lv 22 Slowpoke and a Lv 23 Slowpoke. The Moomoo Farm is next to Norman, you'll find a berry tree which yields 5 Rawst Berries amongst the Miltank. Beside the building on the northwest corner you'll find TM31 (Brick Break). Head into that building to find out Moomoo the Miltank is sick, talk to it five times to feed it 5 oran berries which will restore it to health. Head to the building next door and talk to the female NPC to be given TM49 (Snatch). You can also buy Moomoo milk from the other NPC in this building. Head out and back to the main path and then go south for a battle with Parasol Lady Ruth who has a Lv 23 Pikachu, Pokefan Derek who has a Lv 22 Pikachu and a Lv 22 Ponyta, and then Sailor Eugene who has a Lv 20 Poliwhirl and a Lv 22 Tauros. At night time you can battle Parasol Lady Jaime who has a Lv 20 Umbreon in front of the patch of grass to the west of these three trainers. Within that same patch of grass a Big Nugget can be found. Head south to enter Olivine City!

Olivine City

As you are entering town, your Rival will talk to you as he's leaving the Gym. He'll tell you that the gym leader is apparently taking care of a sick Pokémon at the lighthouse, hinting us where our next objective is. Following the path in front of the Pokémart and then north, you'll find a trainer who will trade you a Voltorb holding a Cheri Berry for a Krabby. In front of the Pokémart is an NPC who can teach your Pokémon Metronome. Head to the Pokécenter towards the center of town to heal up.

Good Rod

From the Pokécenter, head to the buidling directly north to talk to a fisherman who will give you the Good Rod.

Olivine Cafe/HM04 (Strength)

The building next door to the Pokécenter is the Olivine Cafe, speak to a sailor in here to be gifted HM04 (Strength). Prepare yourself for a gauntlet of trainers and head to the lighthouse on the southeast edge of town!

Olivine Lighthouse

The first floor has a couple NPCs you can talk to. On the second floor you will battle Gentleman Alfred who has a Lv 22 Noctowl, and then Sailor Huey who has a Lv 20 Poliwhirl and a Lv 22 Machop. On the third floor you will battle Bird Keeper Theo who has a Lv 23 Murkrow, and then Gentleman Preston who has a Lv 22 Growlithe and a Lv 22 Vulpix. On the fourth floor you will battle Sailor Kent who has a Lv 23 Shellder and a Level 23 Chinchou, followed by Lass Connie who has a Lv 21 Ponyta and a Lv 22 Weepinbell. On the fifth floor Bird Keeper Denis who has a Lv 24 Pidgeotto and a Lv 24 Fearow. You'll also find a Rare Candy and a Hyper Potion on the floor. Drop down the hole to the north of the hyper potion to fall back down to the fourth floor, and then fall down the hole to the east of the sailor for a battle with Sailor Terrell who has a Lv 24 Poliwhirl. There is an Ether on the floor. Head up two sets of stairs to battle Sailor Ernest who has a Lv 22 Machop, a Lv 24 Poliwhirl, and a Lv 24 Quagsire. You'll also find TM34 (Shock Wave) on the ground. Take the final set of stairs to end up at the summit of the lighthouse where Leader Jasmine asks you to get some medicine from the pharmacy in Cianwood. You'll also find a Protein on the ground. Heal up and head west towards Route 40!

Route 40

Krabby — 40% (Lv 40)
Corsola — 15% (Lv 40)
Kingler — 1% (Lv 40)

Another member of the Weekday Family, Monica, is standing by the fence on the beach, answer her question with "Monday" to be given a Sharp Beak. There is a gate just west of Monica that leads to Trainer Hill.You can press that link to check out the section that explains what is going on there. Just know that you won't have access to everything inside until a later point in the game. For now, it is worth noting that there is a Move Tutor inside the gate which can teach your Pokémon Substitute. Starting on the eastern edge of the beach, surf to start a battle with Swimmer Paula who has a Lv 25 Staryu and a Lv 26 Shellder. To the east is Swimmer Simon who has two Lv 25 Tentacools. Swimming south is Swimmer Elaine who has a Lv 25 Starmie. Just south, you'll battle Swimmer Randall who has a Lv 25 Shellder and a Lv 25 Wartortle. Continue south to reach Route 41.

Route 41

Encounter List LandRock Smash/HeadbuttSurfOld RodGood RodSuper Rod
Tentacool — 64% (Lv 20-24)
Horsea — 31% (Lv 21-25)
Mantine — 5% (Lv 22-26)		Magikarp — 70% (Lv 10)

Shellder — 40% (Lv 40)
Lanturn — 15% (Lv 40)
Tentacruel — 5% (Lv 40)

This is a large route with multiple islands currently inaccessible without Whirlpool. We'll start by heading east at the first fork where you will battle Swimmer Charlie who has a Lv 26 Cloyster and a Lv 26 Tentacruel. Just south of Charlie, facing a shoreline, is Swimmer Kara who has a Lv 25 Horsea and a Lv 26 Starmie. Continuing south, you'll battle Swimmer Kirk who has two Lv 24 Gyarados. Continue south between the beach and the rocks to the east to battle Swimmer Wendy who has a Lv 26 Horsea and a Lv 27 Seadra. Continue all the way south and then head east to battle Swimmer George who has a Lv 26 Tentacool, a Lv 26 Remoraid, and a Lv 26 Staryu. Head all the way east, and then north to battle Swimmer Susie who has a Lv 27 Psyduck and a Lv 26 Seaking. Continue north (while hugging the rocks to the west) to find Swimmer Kaylee who has a Lv 24 Seaking, a Lv 24 Lanturn, and a Lv 24 Quagsire. Continue north as far as you can before heading west to battle Swimmer Berke who has a Lv 27 Quilfish. Surf west past the entrance to the route, as well as the first swimmer on the route, to battle Swimmer Denise who has a Lv 27 Lapras. Continue west as far as you can, then head south until you reach the beach of Cianwood City. Heal up at the Pokécenter and then surf west off the southwest corner of Cianwood to battle Swimmer Mathew who has a Lv 26 Krabby and a Lv 26 Quilfish. That's all the trainers, let's head back to Cianwood.

Cianwood City

Cianwood City is another small town. In the house next door to the Pokécenter, and NPC tells you that your Rival scared him into giving away his prized Pokémon and asks you to look after his other one. Say yes to acquire Shuckie, a Lv 20 Shuckle holding a Berry Juice.

Cianwood City Pharmacy

Directly south of the house where you acquire Shuckle is the Cianwood City Pharmacy. Talk to the pharmacist to get the Secret Potion. We will take this back to the lighthouse to heal Amphy. But first, let's finish up things in Cianwood.

North Cianwood/Suicune Sighting 1

Heading to the northern part of the island will lead to Suicune. It will run away as you approach and Mystery Man Eusine will appear and challenge you to a battle to earn Suicune's respect. Eusine has a Lv 27 Politoed, a Lv 27 Hypno, and a Lv 27 Electrode. Ignore the cave to the east for now and heal up, it's time to take on the Cianwood gym!

Cianwood City Gym/HM02 (Fly)

You'll see boulders blocking the path to Chuck as you enter the gym. Head to the west stairs to battle Black Belt Yoshi who has a Lv 29 Hitmonlee, move the boulder one space to the east. Heading up the east stairs starts a battle with Black Belt Lao who has a Lv 29 Hitmonchan. Continuing on will lead to a battle with Black Belt Lung who has a Lv 27 Mankey, a Lv 27 Machoke, and a Lv 29 Primeape. Push the boulder as far west as you can and then continue to battle Black Belt Nob who has a Lv 27 Machop, and a Lv 27 Machoke. Push one more boulder to reach Leader Chuck who has a Lv 30 Hitmontop, a Lv 29 Primeape, a Lv 30 Sudowoodo, and a Lv 31 Poliwrath holding a Black Belt. You'll be rewarded the Stormbadge which makes all Pokémon up to Lv 70 obey and also allows you to use Fly outside of battle. You'll also be given TM01 (Focus Punch). As you exit the gym, Chuck's wife will give you HM02 (Fly). Now it will be much easier to travel to places previously visited! Let's use it now to return to Olivine and get that medicine to the lighthouse.

Olivine City Lighthouse Revisited

Head back to the top floor and Jasmine will administer the Secret Potion to Amphy, who will get better. We can now take on the Olivine Gym! However, as you leave the lighthouse you'll get a call from Warden Baoba alerting you that the Safari Zone has opened! Let's fly back to Cianwood and check that out before taking Jasmine on.

Cliff Edge/Route 47

Head into the cave on the west side of Cianwood, the path will lead you to Route 47. Your first battle will be with Hiker Devin who has two Lv 26 Dunsparce and a Lv 27 Dudunsparce. Head west across the bridge to battle Camper Grant who has a Lv 26 Skiploom and a Lv 26 Marill. On the ground you'll find a Revive. Head into the cave and take the ladder that goes up, once you exit, you'll have a battle with Young Couple Thom & Kae who has a Lv 26 Electabuzz and a Lv 26 Magmar. Continue across a couple of bridges, you'll find a White Flute on the ground. Up ahead is Young Couple Duff & Eda who has a Lv 26 Onix and a Lv 26 Cloyster. Just north is a berry tree which yields 5 Sitrus Berries. Continue on to reach Route 48.

Nothing on this route aside from some new encounters. Continue north to reach the Safari Gate.

As you enter the Safari Gate, you'll notice an NPC to the west who sells Blue Incense and Lax Incense. These items are important for breeding certain baby Pokémon. The merchant on the east side of camp sells vitamins. Head north towards the Safari Zone.

As you enter the building, Baoba will tell you that 2 out of the 6 possible Safari Zones are currently open and will give you the PokeBlock Case. You'll see multiple NPCs around Berry Blending machines which can be used to create PokeBlocks. Pokéblocks are colorful candy blocks made for Pokémon and are primarily used to increase a Pokémon's condition for Pokémon Contests. There are two Pokémon, Feebas and Roselia, who will only evolve after their Beauty is increased to a specific level. The Safari Zone will cost ₽500 to enter, you'll be given 30 safari balls to catch Pokémon. We will get more phone calls from Baoba when the other portions of the Safari Zone open up. Once you're finished here, fly back to Olivine and make sure you are healed up, it's finally time to take on Jasmine!

Olivine City Gym

We already battled the gym trainers in the lighthouse, so the only battle will be with Leader Jasmine who has a Lv 35 Skarmory, a Lv 36 Scizor holding a Focus Band, a Lv 35 Magneton, a Lv 36 Steelix holding a Quick Claw, and a Lv 35 Corsola. You'll be awarded the Mineralbadge which raises your Pokémon's special defense. You'll also be given TM23 (Iron Tail). Our next objective will be to backtrack and collect a multitude of items previously locked behind different HMs.

Surf/Rock Smash/Strength Locations Revisited

Ecruteak Burned Tower (Strength)

You'll find TM12 (Taunt) behind a boulder in the basement.

Route 35 (Surf)

On Route 35, surf across the pond to find berry tree which yields 5 Persim Berries. You'll also find a Big Nugget up ahead on the ground.

Route 34 (Surf)

Surfing on the west side of the route will lead to a Rare Candy on the ground. Surf further south to have a battle with Cool Trainers Jenn & Irene who have a Lv 24 Staryu, a Lv 22 Goldeen, a Lv 26 Starmie, and a Lv 24 Seaking. Next up will be Cool Trainer Kate who has a Lv 26 Shellder and a Lv 28 Cloyster. You'll find Soft Sand on the ground.

Slowpoke Well (Strength, Surf)

Move the boulder on the west side of the well, you'll find a Super Potion on the ground. Surf and then grab the Full Heal on the ground before taking the ladder down. Speaking to the Slowking down here will give you TM18 (Rain Dance), you'll also find a King's Rock on the ground.

Union Cave (Surf)/Lapras

Enter Union Cave from the south (Azalea) and head up and around the first bend, and then surfing across a small patch of water to the south and take the ladder down. You'll battle Pokemaniac Andrew who has two Lv 24 Marowak, surf across the water to battle Pokemaniac Calvin who has a Lv 26 Kangaskhan. Head down the ladder and surf west to find an Ether on the ground. Surf south and towards the west to battle Cooltrainer Nick who has a Lv 26 Charmander, a Lv 26 Bulbasaur, and a Lv 26 Squirtle, there is a Hyper Potion on the ground next to him. Head south to battle Cooltrainer Emma who has a Lv 28 Poliwhirl. Continuing south and then surfing to the east will lead to a stationary Lv 20 Lapras. Head back up to the water to the north, and then surf south to find Cooltrainer Gwen who has a Lv 26 Eevee, a Lv 23 Flareon, a Lv 24 Vaporeon, and a Lv 25 Jolteon, you'll find a Max Revive on the ground next to her. Head back up to the main floor of Union Cave.

Union Cave (Strength, Surf)/Omanyte Puzzle

Once back on the main floor, surf across the water and take the ladder to the north. Surf across the water and move the boulder to find TM43 (Secret Power) on the ground. To the west is Hiker Phillip who has two Lv 23 Geodudes, and a Lv 23 Graveler. To the south is Hiker Leonard who has a Lv 23 Geodude and a Lv 25 Machop. Move the boulder and take the southern exit out to battle Psychic Nathan who has a Lv 26 Girafarig. Jump down the first ledge and enter the ruin to the south to find the Omanyte Puzzle. Solving this will cause you to fall into the main Ruin of Alph, you'll find the Helix Fossil on the ground. Resurrect the Lv 20 Omanyte, put it at the head of your party, and interact with the sensor at the back of the Omanyte Puzzle room to reach the hidden room, you'll find a Lum Berry, Water Stone, Star Piece, and a Stardust. Fall down the hole to the north to see this message spelled out with Unown characters: "They possess great insight and refuse the outside world." Drop down the hole to end up in the main Ruin of Alph once again.

Ruins of Alph (Surf)/Aerodactyl Puzzle

Head out of the main ruin and surf across the water to the south where you will find the Aerodactyl Puzzle room. Solving the puzzle will drop you into the main ruin once again, you'll find the Old Amber on the ground. Resurrect the Lv 20 Aerodactyl and take it back to the puzzle room to open up the back room where you will find a Sitrus Berry, Moon Stone, Energy Root, and a Heal Powder. Fall down the hold to the north to see this message: "Thus we shall erect a Pokémon statue outside." Drop down the hole to end up in the main Ruin of Alph once more.

Union Cave (Strength, Surf)/Ho-oh Puzzle

Head back to Union cave and take the exit opposite of the one which led to the Omanyte Puzzle. You'll battle Super Nerd Stan who has a Lv 20 Grimer. The ruin ahead contains the Ho-oh Puzzle. Complete this one to drop into the main ruin and grab the GS Ball! It is important to note that the GS Ball can be used as a Pokeball (second only to the Master Ball in catch chance), however, it is also a necessary item to possess in order to trigger a legendary encounter. We will do that soon, so be sure not to use the GS Ball before then! While we can't unlock the back room quite yet, eventually you can bring Ho-oh here and grab a Max Revive, Sacred Ash, Charcoal, and a Lum Berry. Drop down the hole to see the final message: "We Unowns must learn to walk in harmony with them. We depart for their sakes."

Violet City (Surf)

Head to Violet City and surf the western pond to find a Hyper Potion on the ground.

Cherrygrove City (Surf)

Fly to Cherrygrove and surf on the water to the west to speak to a fisherman who will give you a Mystic Water.

Tohjo Falls

Fly to New Bark Town and surf on the water to the east. Enter the cave opening to Tohjo Falls and surf west to find a Heart Scale.

Azalea Town/Celebi

Fly to Azalea Town and talk to Kurt who will check out the GS Ball. As he does this, we'll hear a sound from the forest and head outside. Kurt will ask you to investigate. Heal up and be sure to stock up on Pokeballs, it's time to snag our first legendary Pokémon! Head into Ilex Forest, cut down the tree, and interact with the shrine to start an encounter with a Lv 40 Celebi. If you miss catching it, don't worry, it will respawn as we get further along into the game.

You can continue heading east to arrive at Mahogany Town to heal up, and then head back to Route 42 and enter the eastern entrance to Mt. Mortar.

When you enter the cave, progress northwards until you see an opening. Enter to head deeper in the mountain. Then, take the stairs ahead to battle Pokemaniac Miller who has a Lv 20 Nidoking and a Lv 20 Nidoqueen. Take the ladder up behind Miller to find an Elixer on the ground and then head back down. Then, head West until you reach the other side of the cave, where you'll find a second ladder. Go up and find a Revive, then head back down. Start heading back East until you see a boulder. Push it and head North until you come to the first split in the path. Head West first and you'll find a Revive, before returning to the crossroads and now heading North again. You find another stairway ahead, climb and find a Nugget on the cliff before returning to the main dirt path again. Continue east at the fork in the path to find an Escape Rope and then jump down two ledges, hugging the eastern wall to eventually find a King's Rock to the south. Turn north again until you see a cliff with stairs to battle Super Nerd Markus, he has a Lv 19 Slowpoke. Turn around again and jump down the visible ledge, retracing your footsteps to the fork in the path again, this time heading north, where you’ll also find an Ultra Ball on the ground. Continue north up the stairs, progressing to the northern stairs down at the next fork before heading south at another fork to find a Max Repel on the ground. From here, return to the previous fork and continue west and then south, ignoring the ladder going down for now, and jump the ledge. Progress on the path here to find a Max Potion on a cliff ahead and then navigate around to begin north again, before taking the upward ladder just North. Be wary, the wild Pokémon on this floor are higher leveled than on the lower levels. Head east until; you reach a fork, heading south to find an Ether on the ground, before returning and continuing on path to until another fork. Here turn south to find an Iron on a cliff. Return to the fork and continue on to find a Max Revive on another cliff. Surf across the water and continue until you reach another fork. Turn south first to find a Rare Candy on yet another cliff. Return to the fork and surf across the next pond and follow the path east, you'll reach another pond to surf across. Stop here to ascend the stairs and find a Dragon Scale on a cliff. Surf again to the south, ignore the exit, and go north up the stairs to battle Super Nerd Hugh who has a Lv 39 Seadra. Now go West and surf across the final pond to find an Elixer on the ground in the southwest corner of the map. Head all the way back to the northwest corner of the map and take the ladder back to the previous floor, and then follow the path east, west at the fork, and around to take the ladder down that we had previously skipped. To the east is the Karate King Black Belt Kiyo who has a Lv 34 Hitmonlee and a Lv 34 Hitmonchan. He will give you a Lv 20 Tyrogue for beating him. Move the boulder to the south and surf east to find a Hyper Potion on the ground. surfing south will lead to a Carbos on a cliff. Continue surfing south to find a Max Ether on a cliff. Surf back to where the boulder is, this time surfing south at the fork, to reach a Max Revive on the ground. Surf south, exit onto the west bank, and then follow the path to find a PP Up. Head back to the water, surf across, and then jump down the ledge and take the ladder up, and then head south to exit Mt. Mortar. Time to head back to Mahogany Town!

Mahogany Town

Encounter List LandRock Smash/HeadbuttSurfOld RodGood RodSuper Rod
Aipom — 90% (Lv 15)
Heracross — 5% (Lv 15)
Pineco — 5% (Lv 15)

Welcome to Mahogany Town! The gym is currently blocked off, and Grandma's Shop is also currently shut down. When trying to exit town to the east, an NPC will block your path and try to sell you Ragecandybars. There's nothing else to do here for now, so head towards the gate to the north which leads to Route 43.

Route 43

Head north to have a battle with Camper Spencer who has a Lv 25 Sandslash and a Lv 25 Golbat. If you attempt to go through the gate up ahead, a Rocket Grunt will extort ₽500 from you to go through. Instead, head into the grass, up ahead you'll battle Picnicker Tiffany who has a Lv 34 Clefable and a Lv 34 Wigglytuff. Surf across the water to find two berry trees which yield 5 Leppa Berries. Note: This is the only place to acquire an infinite amount of a PP restoring item, so be sure to remember where this tree is. On the ground is a Max Ether. Surf east back across the water and head north through the grass to battle Pokemaniac Ben who has a Lv 26 Slowbro and a Lv 26 Wartortle. Head south and then east to reach the other side of the Rocket gate, and then head north to battle Pokemaniac Ron who has a Lv 26 Nidoking and a Lv 26 Charmeleon. Next, you'll battle Pokemaniac Brent who has a Lv 26 Lickitung and a Lv 26 Ivysaur. Head back to the water and surf north to battle Fisherman Marvin who has two Lv 25 Gyarados. Take path the east of the water in front of Marvin and head north to reach the Lake of Rage.

Lake of Rage

Encounter List LandRock Smash/HeadbuttSurfOld RodGood RodSuper Rod
Pineco — 60% (Lv 20)
Exeggcute — 30% (Lv 20)
Venonat — 10% (Lv 20)		Magikarp — 99% (Lv 10-13)
Gyarados — 1% (Lv 10-19)		Magikarp — 100% (Lv 10)Magikarp — 80% (Lv 20)
Gyarados — 20% (Lv 40)		Gyarados — 99% (Lv 40)
Magikarp — 1% (Lv 100)

Talk to Fisherman Andre to battle, he has a Lv 27 Gyarados. Follow the path on the eastern shore of the lake to find another member of the Weekday Family Wesley, answer his question with "Wednesday" to be awarded a Black Belt. Just north, you'll find a Red Flute on the ground. Surf onto the lake to battle a Lv 30 Shiny Gyarados, you'll receive a Red Scale afterwards. Return to the shore and talk to the NPC with a Dragonite who will introduce himself as Lance. He will ask you to help with an investigation, agree, and he will head back towards Mahogany. Before heading there to help, surf through the small channel heading west on the south side of the Lake. You'll battle Cooltrainer Lois who has a Lv 28 Skiploom and a Lv 27 Ninetales. Note: Lois will only appear if you have already battled the Shiny Gyarados. To the west is Fisherman Raymond who has a Lv 28 Vaporeon. Surf back into the channel, this time heading north around a bend, head east at the branch, and then take the middle branch which will eventually lead to a house. The NPC inside will give you TM10 (Hidden Power), just east of the house you'll find a Full Restore. Surf back to the three-way branch, this time taking the east path, and then heading north to battle Cooltrainer Aaron who has a Lv 27 Ivysaur, a Lv 27 Charmeleon, and a Lv 27 Wartortle. You'll find a Max Revive on the ground. That's it for now at the Lake of Rage, so fly back to Mahogany, heal up, and head into Grandma's Shop.

Mahogany Rocket Hideout/HM08 (Whirlpool)

Encounter List LandRock Smash/HeadbuttSurfOld RodGood RodSuper Rod
Voltorb — 40% (Lv 21)
Geodude — 30% (Lv 21)
Koffing — 30% (Lv 21)

As you enter Grandma's Shop, you'll see Lance's Dragonite Hyper Beam a Rocket Grunt (are we the baddies?). Follow Lance down into the basement. As you walk in front of the statue, you'll be ambushed by two Rocket Grunts, the first has a Lv 26 Drowzee and a Lv 26 Gligar, the second has a Lv 27 Golbat, a Lv 27 Ariados, and a Lv 27 Raticate. These two grunts will conitnue to ambush you as you pass statues unless you flip a hidden switch. This is a good source for extra xp and money if needed. You'll find the switch by heading south at the first branch, south at the second branch, and then interacting with the vent on the wall. Continue south around the bend to battle Collector Jed who has a Lv 28 Magnemite and a Lv 28 Porygon. There is a Big Nugget on the ground. Head back around the bend into the wider hallway and follow the path east. You'll find a Hyper Potion on the ground. Follow that path towards the staircase in the southwestern corner, avoiding the grey teleportation tile, and head north onto the yellow square where you can have wild Pokémon encounters, and will find a Revive on the ground. Continue north to battle another Rocket Grunt who has a Lv 28 Kangaskhan. Head back south and take the stairs to the lower level. You'll meet up with Lance who heals your Pokémon before continuing on into the hideout. Head east to battle a Rocket Grunt who has a Lv 29 Golbat. By the staircase is another Rocket Grunt who has a Lv 26 Raticate, a Lv 26 Golbat, and a Lv 27 Grimer. Take the staircase down to meet up again with Lance, he will tell you that it takes two passwords to enter the boss's room, and that only Rocket Grunts would know the passwords. Head north into the mostly enclosed area to battle Collector Ross who has a Lv 28 Koffing and a Lv 28 Raichu. Just north is the first Rocket Grunt who knows a password, she has a Lv 28 Arbok and a Lv 28 Sneasle. Talk to her once more after the battle to get the first password. Head out of this area and to the southwestern enclosure to battle Collector Mitch who has a Lv 28 Electrode. Continue east to battle Rocket Grunt Eto who has a Lv 28 Jynx, a Lv 28 Magmar, a Lv 28 Poliwhirl, and a Lv 28 Electabuzz. Talk to him once more after the battle to get the second password. You'll find a Guard Spec and a Full Heal on the ground. Head out of this area and then go north to find an Ice Heal on the ground. Head east towards the staircase, hug the eastern wall and go south to find a Protein on the ground. Head back up and head upstairs. Head east along the northern hall to battle a Rocket Grunt who has a Lv 31 Venomoth. Head down the stairs here, and then take the staircase to the south up to find TM46 (Thief) on the ground. Head back down the stairs and around the corner to have a short cutscene with your Rival, and then grab the Hyper Potion on the ground. Use the passwords to open up the shutters to battle Rocket Admin Petrel who has a Lv 30 Pineco, a Lv 30 Magcargo, a Lv 30 Cloyster, and a Lv 30 Larvitar. Follow the Murkrow to back to the second floor of the hideout where it will mimic Petrel's voice to open up the final shutter. Attempting to go forward will lead to a double battle in which we team up with Lance against Rocket Admin Ariana & Rocket Grunt, they have a Lv 31 Arbok, two Lv 25 Golbats, a Lv 30 Arbok, a Lv 30 Vileplume, and a Lv 30 Persian. After this, Lance will tell you that we have to knock out the Electrodes connected to the machine, so either defeat or catch all three Lv 30 Electrodes. Lance will then give you HM08 (Whirlpool) and leave, we will do the same. Upstairs, the shop is now open and offers items you won't find anywhere else! Exit the shop and head north out of town to Route 43. Walk into the gate to the west that was previously guarded by Rocket Grunts. Speak to the guard in here to receive TM36 (Sludge Bomb). Fly back to Mahogany Town and get healed up, we are going to challenge the Mahogany Gym!

Mahogany Town Gym

The gimmick in this gym is it's icy floors. To start, align yourself with the rock to the east and skate towards it. Skate north from here to battle Expert Donald who has a Lv 29 Dewgong and a Lv 30 Delibird. Skate west and then north to reach solid ground. At the end of this path, skate west and then north to battle Expert Douglas who has a Lv 28 Shellder, a Lv 28 Seel, and a Lv 30 Cloyster. Skate west from here to battle Expert Brad who has a Lv 30 Swinub and a Lv 30 Sneasel. Skate north into a rock, and then west to battle Expert Clarissa who has a Lv 31 Dewgong. Skate south into a rock, and then east to battle Expert Roxanne who has a Lv 30 Jynx. Skate north from here to arrive at Leader Pryce, he has a Lv 37 Cloyster, a Lv 37 Sneasel, a Lv 37 Jynx, a Lv 37 Dewgong, and a Lv 38 Mamoswine holding a Sitrus Berry. Upon victory you will be awarded the Glacierbadge which raises the special attack of your Pokémon and allows you to use Whirlpool outside of battle. You'll also be given TM07 (Hail). As you exit the gym, you'll receive a phone call from Prof. Elm informing you that the radio broadcasts are talking about Team Rocket, and asks if you know anything about it. Our next big objective will be to investigate the Radio Tower in Goldenrod. Note: As of V1.2.1, if you haven't yet made your way to Route 39 (west of Ecruteak), you must do so before taking on the events in Goldenrod. Failure to do so will result in a progression blocking bug. This will be fixed in the future!

Goldenrod Radio Tower Rocket Takeover

Fly to Goldenrod and heal up at the Pokécenter if needed. We are about to face a gauntlet of Rockets. Head to east onto the main road and then go north. Take the first white path that goes west and enter the Radio Tower. A Rocket Grunt will be blocking the stairway on the northeast corner of this room, talk to him to start a battle, he has a three Lv 33 Raticates. Take the stairs to the second floor, hug the northern wall, and go east to battle a Rocket Grunt with a Lv 34 Gligar and a Lv 34 Hypno. Directly south will be another Rocket Grunt with a Lv 33 Weezing and a Lv 33 Muk. Continue west and then talk to the Rocket Grunt in front of the wall to the east of the stairs, he has a two Lv 34 Aipoms and a Lv 34 Koffing. Walk in front of this grunt to catch the eyes of another Rocket Grunt who has a Lv 35 Arbok and a Lv 35 Gloom. Ascend the stairs above to the third floor, and head east to battle another Rocket Grunt who has a Lv 34 Aipom, a Lv 34 Murkrow, and a Lv 34 Forretress. Talk to the NPC by the first table south of this Grunt to battle Collector Marc who has a Lv 37 Omastar. East of Marc is another Rocket Grunt who has a Lv 34 Muk and a Lv 34 Venomoth. Take the staircase east of the first Grunt we battled on this floor to the 4th floor. Head south into a table and then east to battle Collector Rich who has a Lv 40 Porygon. Continue east and talk to the Rocket Grunt to start a battle, he has a Lv 35 Exeggutor, a Lv 35 Electabuzz, and a Lv 35 Electrode. Head north until you hit a plant, and then east to battle another Rocket Grunt, she has a Lv 36 Arbok, a Lv 35 Gloom, a Lv 36 Ariados, and a Lv 36 Vileplume. Ascend the staircase in the northeast corner to reach the final floor. Talk to the "director" to reveal he is actually Rocket Admin Petrel in disguise, he has a Lv 38 Forretress, a Lv 39 Magcargo, a Lv 38 Cloyster, and a Lv 38 Pupitar. Upon victory, Petrel will inform you that the Rockets have stashed the real director in the Goldenrod Underground and will give you the Basement Key. Head all the way back downstairs and make a pit stop at the Pokécenter.

Goldenrod Underground/Rival Battle 4

The entrance to the Underground is the building to the west as you exit the Pokécenter, you get there by heading north onto the main road and taking the first path east and following that around a bend until you reach the only building on this road not blocked by Rocket Grunts. Head inside and take the staircase down into the Underground. Head north until the path branches, take the eastern path, and you'll meet another Kimono Girl. Interact with the door to the north to use the Basement Key to open it up, and enter. As you try to head west, your Rival will stop you for another battle, his possible teams are:
