After the battle with your Rival, we will work ourselves toward the western wall, battling three Grunts along the way. The first Rocket Grunt has a Lv 36 Golbat and a Lv 37 Muk. The next Rocket Grunt has a Lv 36 Golbat and a Lv 37 Arbok. The final Rocket Grunt has a Lv 36 Golbat and a Lv 37 Weezing. After battling this last grunt, interact with the switch north of him. This opens the shutter directly to the south. Head through the now-opened path and battle Burglar Duncan, he has a Lv 35 Delibird and a Lv 35 Magmar. Head south to have another battle, this time with Burglar Eddie who has a Lv 35 Arcanine and a Lv 35 Weezing. You'll find a Smoke Ball on the ground. Head back up to the northern path, this time hitting the switch behind the second Grunt. This opens the shutter directly south, head through the opening to find a Max Revive. Head back to the northern path one more time and hit the switch behind the first Grunt, this opens two shutters. Head south from the center switch towards the bottom of the room and then go east, collecting the Max Potion on the ground. Just past this item you'll see a staircase guarded by a Rocket Grunt who has a Lv 35 Vileplume, a Lv 35 Arbok, a Lv 36 Murkrow, and a Lv 35 Pinsir. Take the staircase down to the next level. Head north to find an Ultra Ball on the northwest corner. From there, head east, south, and then hug the wall as you turn east to run into a Rocket Grunt who has a Lv 37 Weezing. Directly south across the hallway is another Rocket Grunt who has a Lv 35 Weezing, a Lv 35 Hypno, and a Lv 37 Gligar. Continue east until you hit the eastern wall to find a Max Ether on the ground. Continue on, ignore the stairs in the northeast corner, and head west then south to battle Rocket Eto, he has a Lv 36 Poliwrath, a Lv 36 Magmar, a Lv 36 Jynx, a Lv 36 Electabuzz, and a Lv 36 Rhydon. You'll see the real director just below Eto, talk to him to receive the Card Key, which can be used to open the shutters on the third floor of the Radio Tower. Grab the TM04 (Calm Mind) on the ground and head back to the northeast corner of the room and ascend the staircase. You'll find yourself in the lower level of the Goldenrod Dept Store. Grab the Amulet Coin west of the staircase and then head south and speak to the NPC to get his Machoke to move the boulder out of your way. Take the elevator up to floor 1, exit the Dept. Store, head to the Pokécenter to heal up, and then make your way back to the Radio Tower.

Goldenrod Radio Tower Finished

Head up to the third floor of the Radio Tower and walk towards the eastern part of this room. Interact with the shutter to use the Card Key to open it, and then head north to battle a Rocket Grunt who has a Lv 35 Tauros and a Lv 35 Slowpoke. Ascend the staircase to the east, and then continue east to battle Rocket Admin Proton who has a Lv 36 Muk, a Lv 36 Weezing, a Lv 37 Nidoqueen, a Lv 37 Nidoking, and a Lv 38 Rhydon. Ascend the staircase to the east to arrive at the final floor. Once more, we will battle Rocket Admin Ariana, she has a Lv 39 Arbok, a Lv 39 Vileplume, a Lv 40 Murkrow, a Lv 40 Gyarados, and a Lv 40 Persian. Just south we will battle the leader of this branch of Team Rocket Rocket Admin Archer. He has a Lv 39 Porygon-Z, a Lv 38 Houndoom, a Lv 39 Gyarados, a Lv 38 Tauros, and a Lv 40 Slowbro. Our victory leads to Archer disbanding Team Rocket once again! The director will return to the room and give you either the Rainbow Wing or the SIlver Wing, depending on what color you told Baoba the bird was earlier on. If you've been following this walkthrough up to this point, you will get the Rainbow Wing. He will tell you the wing is supposedly connected to a legendary Pokémon, and that another item is apparently needed to summon that Pokémon. Take the staircase down and talk to Mary on the next floor, she will give you Brightpowder. Descend down the western staircase, exit the enclosure towards the main room, and talk to the female NPC sitting at a table to the south to be given TM48 Skill Swap. That is it for the events in Goldenrod! Heal up and Fly towards Mahogany Town and then head east out of town to reach Route 44.

Route 44

Route 44 is pretty straightforward, the main path encircles the entire route, with the exit being towards the southeast corner. To start, head east into the route and then take the southern path. Just before a bridge is Bird Keeper Vance who has a Lv 36 Pidgeot, a Lv 37 Skarmory, a Lv 36 Murkrow, and a Lv 36 Xatu. Continue east onto a bridge where you can battle Fisherman Edgar, he has a Lv 38 Octillery, a Lv 36 Seaking, and a Lv 36 Gyarados. Continuing east will least to Cooltrainer Cybil who has a Lv 40 Butterfree, a Lv 37 Bellossom, and a Lv 38 Quagsire. Rather than following the road to the staircase, take the path heading north between trees, and then head east through a small gap through the trees into some grass bordering water, you'll find an Elixer on the ground. Head back east to the northbound path between trees and continue north, this leads to Pokemaniac Zach who has a Lv 40 Rhydon, a Lv 38 Heracross, and a Lv 37 Pupitar. Heading west, you'll see Fisherman Wilton by the water, he has a Lv 38 Quilfish, a Lv 36 Octillery, and a Lv 36 Seaking. Continue west to find an Ultra Ball on the ground. Just below that Ultra Ball is Psychic Phil who has a Lv 36 Xatu, a Lv 36 Girafarig, and a Lv 36 Kadabra. South of Phil is a berry tree which yields 5 Aspear Berries. You'll find yourself at the beginning of the route, take the southern path east to that staircase we previously ignored and head up the stairs to battle Cooltrainer Allen who has a Lv 35 Charmeleon and a Lv 37 Electabuzz. You'll find a Max Repel on the ground in the southeast corner. From there, head north into the cave opening to reach the Ice Path.

Ice Path/HM07 (Waterfall)

As the name suggests, we will have some ice patches to navigate as we progress through this cave. Head east onto some ice, go south, east, south, and then east to get past the first patch of ice. Following the path will lead to a much larger ice patch. Skate north, west, north, east, south, west, south, west, north, east, south, west, north, west, south, east, south, and finally east to get past this ice. Head east towards a smaller ice patch and skate east, north, west, south, and then east to find HM07 (Waterfall). Jump the small ledge and head east to descend down a ladder. Descend the ladder on the southwest corner of the next room. Take one step east, and then skate north to find a Full Heal on solid ground and then skate east to find a Carbos on some more solid ground. Skate south, and then west to arrive where we started in this room and ascend the ladder back up. Ignoring the ladder, you'll see some ice to the north which has cracks in it. To traverse across these cracks, we will need to use the speed setting on our bike. To shift gears into speed mode, you'll mount your bike and press R while stationary. The bike needs at least two steps to get up to speed, and any patch of normal ice you hit will slow you down enough to fall through the cracks in the ice. Align yourself at least two steps south from any of the three cracks, mount your bike, press R once, and head north across the ice. Align yourself with the center crack to the east, ensuring you are two steps away, and bike east across the ice and into a rock 3 steps onto solid ground. This next part can be a tad bit tricky, you'll need to be prepared to head east as soon as you hit solid ground, otherwise you will cause the ice below to break and will have to start from square one. The picture included shows what is going on below, and where you need to head east. Once you've successfully navigated that patch of ice, continue east into the wall, south into a rock, and then bike west across the final patch of ice on this floor. From here, head north and drop into the hole in the ground. Grab the Max Potion on the ground before descending the ladder. On the next floor, head east and use Rock Smash to break the rock south to find a Nevermeltice. Hop the ledge to the east and head north to ascend a ladder. On the next floor, you'll head east and skate south across a tiny patch of ice. Before jumping the ledge onto the larger patch of ice, align yourself with the staircase, then head south. You'll see a Delibird to the east, skate towards it and give it an Aspear Berry to receive TM14 (Blizzard). Skate west across the ice and ascend the ladder. To the east, you'll find an Iron. Skate south into a rock, east onto solid ground, and walk down to the spot two spaces from away from the southern wall. Skate west into a rock, south, and then west to reach solid ground. Skate south, west, and then south once more to reach a Revive. Skate east, north, and then east to the patch of solid ground before the larger patch of ice. From here, skate east, north, then east to reach the ground on the eastern wall. Walk down two steps, skate west, north, and then west once more to reach solid ground, before ascending the ladder to the north. On the next floor you will find a PP Up on a cliff to the north. Align yourself with the rock on the ice and skate south, west, south, and then east to end up behind a Kimono Girl. Talk to here and respond with "Yes" to push her off the ice. Skate back north, east, south, west, north, east, south, east, and then south to find a Protein. Skate north, east, north, west, south, east, and then south to arrive back on solid ground. The exit is south from here!

Blackthorn City

Encounter List LandRock Smash/HeadbuttSurfOld RodGood RodSuper Rod
Aipom — 90% (Lv 15)

Welcome to Blackthorn City! As you exit the ice path, the first house to the west has an NPC who will trade you a Mr. Mime holding a Smoke Ball for a Dodrio. Head west from this house to the edge of the ledge to the north, head north, and then east to find a chubby NPC in front of a large boulder. This is Santos, another member of the Weekday Family, answer his question with "Saturday" to receive a Spell Tag. Head south until you find yourself in front of the Pokécenter, then head west towards a sign that reads "MOVE BROS HOUSE". Enter the house to find the Move Deleter and the Move Tutor. The Deleter is obsolete in H&S due to HM moves being forgettable. The Move Tutor will teach a Pokémon any moves it could have already learned in exchange for a Heart Scale. Oftentimes, there are moves a Pokémon learns at Lv 1 that we never see due to them being overwritten by moves learned while leveling up, so be sure and check to see if there are any hidden surprises in the learnset of whatever Pokémon you are using in your party. You can also refer to the Pokedex to see a Pokémon's learnset, which is the list of moves that the Move Tutor pulls from. A bridge to the south leads to Route 45 if you require extra training or want to catch new Pokémon. But for now, let's head north to take on our final gym in Johto, the Blackthorn City Gym!

Blackthorn City Gym

As you enter the gym, you'll notice what looks like 3 blue/gray rocks hanging over a lava pit. As you approach these, a bridge will appear. Hug the east side of the bridge and head north to battle Cool Trainer Fran who has a Lv 41 Seadra, a Lv 41 Dragonaire, and a Lv 41 Gyarados. To the west is Dragon Tamer Cody who has a Lv 42 Seadra, a Lv 41 Dragonaire, a Lv 42 Dragonaire, and a Lv 43 Golduck. Continue north and east across the next bridge, and then head north to battle Dragon Tamer Paul who has a Lv 34 Dratini and three Lv 40 Dragonaires. Head north and then west across another bridge, then continue along the path. The western trainer is Dragon Tamer Mike who has a Lv 42 Gyarados, a Lv 42 Vaporeon, and a Lv 43 Dragonaire. The trainer just east is Cool Trainer Lola who has a Lv 41 Dragonaire, a Lv 42 Mantine, a Lv 41 Lanturn, and a Lv 42 Dragonaire. Head north across the bridge to reach Leader Claire, she has a Lv 42 Dragonaire, a Lv 43 Gyarados, a Lv 44 Dudunsparce, a Lv 43 Lapras, and a Lv 45 Kingdra holding Leftovers. Rather than handing over her badge, Claire will insist that you must take the dragon user challenge in the Dragon's Den to the north of the gym. Heal up if necessary and then surf north to enter the Den.

Dragon's Den

Descend the ladder down and then exit south to reach the Den. Head south to battle Dragon Tamer Darin who has four Lv 42 Dragonaires. Surf south, and then west under the whirlpool, through a channel between two rocks, and disembark onto land to the north. Just ahead is Cool Trainer Cara who has a Lv 40 Seadra, a Lv 41 Seadra, and a Lv 42 Gyarados. Surf south from here and disembark on the land to the west to battle Twins Lea & Pia, they have two Lv 41 Dragonaires, and two Lv 42 Gyarados. Just south of the twins is a Max Potion. Surf east and then south through a whirlpool, continuing south for a short distance before heading east and then north. You'll disembark onto a boardwalk and head into the building. Once inside, the Dragon Elder will ask you a few questions. If answered correctly, you'll be awarded a very special Pokémon soon after. The correct answers are: Friend, Training, Anybody, Love, and Both. Claire will enter the house and ask how things went, after being admonished by the Elder, she will finally hand over the Risingbadge. This badge enables you to use Waterfall outside of battle and causes all Pokémon to obey you, regardless of level. Exit the house and surf east. On the first patch of land, you'll find a PP Max. Surf north from here to arrive at another patch of land where you will find a Dragon Fang. Surf south, west, and north back through the whirlpool we previously traversed and then surf northwest until you arrive at the beginning of the Den. Here, Claire will give you TM02 (Dragon Claw), and tell you to head back towards New Bark Town in order to reach the league. Before leaving the Den, surf back to the house and speak to the Elder once more to be given a Shiny Lv 15 Dratini which knows Extremespeed, assuming you answered the questions correctly. Surf back to the entrance and head back up to Blackthorn City. As you exit the Den, you will receive a phone call from Elm asking you to see him at his lab. Rather than flying straight there, heal up, and then head south across a bridge out of Blackthorn towards Route 45.

Dark Cave North

First, we are going to revisit the Dark Cave via an entrance at the top of Route 45. Head south and then west across a bridge, you'll see the entrance to Dark Cave to the north. Head inside the cave and use Flash to illuminate your surroundings. Begin by heading west and then south to reach some water. Begin surfing south while hugging the wall to the west, surf west as soon as you can, and then surf north onto the first bank of solid ground. Continue north up a set of stairs and head west around a corner. Continue west, ignoring the path to the south, until you find an NPC just north of another set of stairs. Talking to him will grant you some Blackglasses. Head back east, ignoring the path to the south once again, and then go south to the water we previously surfed. Surf and then disembark onto the bank to the south, heading south between two pairs of large boulders, and then head east across the southern face of the eastern pair. You'll see some stairs leading up to a cliff to the north where you will find a Star Piece. Head back down the stairs and the go west until you find a Black Flute just east of a ledge. Head back east past the cliff where we found the Star Piece and then surf north across the water. Disembark to the north to find yourself where we entered the cave, continue north and then east to exit back onto Route 45.

After exiting Dark Cave, head west and then south to battle Hiker Erik who has a Lv 37 Machoke, a Lv 37 Kangaskhan, and a Lv 38 Golem. Head back north, east across a bridge, and then south into some grass to battle Cool Trainer Kelly. She has a Lv 37 Togetic, a Lv 37 Tangela, a Lv 37 Ampharos, and a Lv 38 Blastoise. Jump the ledge to the south and then head west to find a Full Heal. Head east through some grass, down some stairs, and then jump a ledge to battle Cool Trainer Ryan. He has a Lv 37 Pidgeot, a Lv 37 Magmar, and a Lv 37 Electabuzz. Jump the ledge to the south to find a Nugget. Go west into some grass to battle Hiker Michael who has a Lv 38 Rhyhorn, a Lv 38 Donphan, and a Lv 38 Golem. Jump down another ledge to the south and continue south into some grass to battle Black Belt Kenji. He has a Lv 33 Onix, a Lv 33 Steelix, a Lv 38 Machamp, and a Lv 36 Hitmonlee. Hug the eastern wall and head south, hopping another ledge, to battle Hiker Timothy. He has two Lv 38 Dugtrios, a Lv 39 Gligar, and a Lv 38 Graveler. Just south you'll find an Elixer. Head west and then north around the Cliff Timothy was standing up against to battle Hiker Parry who has a Lv 45 Piloswine, a Lv 45 Dugtrio, and a Lv 48 Steelix. Head north, west across a bridge, and the north through some grass to find a Max Potion. Head south through two patches of grass and down some stairs to find Camper Quentin in some grass. He has a Lv 38 Fearow, a Lv 38 Primeape, a Lv 38 Raichu, and a Lv 38 Tauros. Head south and cross the first bridge you see to the east to find a berry tree which yields 5 Aspear Berries. Head back west across the bridge and continue west to reach Route 46.

Dark Cave: Final Visit

As you enter Route 46, you'll see another cave opening leading to Dark Cave to the north. Enter, use Flash, and grab an Elixer just east of the opening. Use rock smash to break the rock to the north and then continue north, hugging the eastern wall, and up three sets of stairs until you can't go further north. Head west until you find a Revive. Head south down two sets of stairs and jump a ledge. Go south down one more set of stairs and then jump over a ledge to find a Max Revive. Jump down one more ledge and exit back out to Route 46 to the south.

Route 46

Head west as you exit Dark Cave to find a berry tree which yields 5 Lum Berries. Head back east towards the entrance to Dark Cave and then jump the ledge to the south. Head west to battle Camper Ted who has a Lv 40 Ursaring, a Lv 40 Primeape, and a Lv 40 Nidoking. Just next to Ted is Picnicker Erin who has two Lv 40 Rapidash, a Lv 38 Nidoqueen, and a Lv 38 Raichu. Head east to hit the treeline and then south to find a Revive. Ignore the ledge to the south and head back east until you see a path going south. Take this path to battle Hiker Bailey who has five Lv 40 Golems. That's it for this route! You can jump the ledge to the south to reach the southern part of Route 46 accessible early in the game, and then head through the gate to end back on Route 29, just west of New Bark Town. Either take this route, or just Fly yourself to New Bark and head to Elm's Lab!

New Bark Town/Master Ball

Talking to Elm inside his lab will result in you being gifted the Master Ball! He will also tell you that the Kimono Girls had been looking for you and that they invite you to meet them at the Ecruteak Dance Theater. We have one quick stop before heading to Ecruteak, so head outside and Fly to the Safari Zone Gate!

Route 47 Revisited/Chansey Farm

From the Safari Zone Gate, head south onto Route 48 and across a bridge, then east across another bridge. Head south onto Route 47 and down some stairs in front of a couple of NPCs. Continue south, west across a bridge, south, east across a bridge, north, east across another bridge, and then head into the cave opening to the north. Inside the cave, take the ladder down, then another ladder down on the next floor, and exit the cave to the south. Surf north up the channel just west of the cave exit, and use Waterfall to climb the waterfall. Surf north, and then disembark on the land to the east. You'll find a Pearl. The grass here is what I refer to as the Chansey Farm, and is a great place to level up your Pokémon if needed. Chansey has a 40% encounter rate in this grass, gives a high amount of exp, and also have a chance to be holding a Lucky Egg, an item that grants 50% more exp to a Pokémon holding it. Chansey have high HP and Special Defense, so be sure and bring a Pokémon who knows some physical moves if you plan to farm levels here! Once you're finished, Fly to Ecruteak City, it's time to see what the Kimono Girls want!

Ecruteak Dance Theater Kimono Girls

Be sure and heal up as you arrive in Ecruteak, and then head to the Dance Theater just north of the Pokécenter. As you try to enter the Dance Theater, your Rival will exit and lament being defeated by the Kimono Girls. You'll have a short conversation with a Kimono Girl before being asked if you're ready to be tested. Respond with yes to start the battles! First up is Kimono Girl Zuki who has a Lv 48 Umbreon. Next is Kimono Girl Naoko who has a Lv 48 Espeon. Following Naoko is Kimono Girl Miki who has a Lv 48 Flareon. Kimono Girl Sayo is up next, she has a Lv 48 Jolteon. Finally, you'll battle Kimono Girl Kuni who has a Lv 48 Vaporeon. Upon defeating all five Kimono Girls, you'll be gifted the either the Clear Bell (Ho-oh) or the Tidal Bell (Lugia).The building will then shake and an NPC will run in and say that they saw the shadow of a large Pokémon either over the Tin Tower (Ho-oh) or the Whirl Islands (Lugia). If you've been following the walkthrough to this point, we will be heading to the Tin Tower. If you chose silver earlier, skip to the section about Lugia here.

Tin Tower/Ho-oh

Encounter List LandRock Smash/HeadbuttSurfOld RodGood RodSuper Rod
Rattata — 65% (Lv 3-5)
Gastly — 35% (Lv 4-6)

From the Pokécenter in Ecruteak, walk north, the entrance leading to the Tin Tower is the second building directly north of the Pokécenter. Enter the building, and talk to the eastermost Sage which will trigger your Clear Bell to ring, causing the Sage to let you through. In the next room, head north and then east out an exit. Rather than following the path, immediately head south, navigating around some trees, to find a Big Mushroom. Head back north to the grey path, and follow it east and slightly south to the entrance of the Tin Tower. Go north after entering the tower and ascend the ladder. In the next room, head south and ascend another ladder. In the next room you'll see 4 ramps surrounding the platform you are on. You can jump these as if they were ledges. Jump over the southwestern ramp to find a Full Heal. Jump back to the initial platform by taking the ramp to the northeast, and then continue straight and jump over one more ramp to reach the floor on the east side of the building. Head north, jump over two ramps going west, walk around the center pillar, and jump two more ramps to the west. Head north and then jump over 5 ramps heading east, and then ascend the ladder in the northeast corner of this room. In the next room, head south and jump over 5 ramps to find an Escape Rope by a ladder. Ignore the ladder and jump over six ramps heading west, you'll find an Ultra Ball by another ladder. Once again, ignore the ladder, walk north and jump over two ramps to a platform in the middle of the room, you'll find a Max Potion here. Jump two ramps on the northwest edge of this platform and ascend the ladder just north, On the next floor, head north and then jump 5 ramps heading east to reach a platform on the northeast corner of this room, you'll find a Carbos. From here, jump three ramps heading south, and then two ramps heading east to reach the middle platform where you will find a Rare Candy. On the southern edge of this platform, jump three ramps heading east, two ramps heading south, and descend the ladder. Jump six ramps heading west, walk north, jump two ramps heading east, walk north, then jump two ramps heading west and ascend the ladder to the north. This next portion can be tricky, so take your time. Walk north from the ladder, jump two ramps going east, one ramp going south, two ramps going west, five ramps heading south, two ramps heading east, then one more ramp heading south and ascend the ladder. On the next floor, head north towards the center of the room to find a Max Potion. Head east, north, west across the room, south, jump two ramps heading south, and ascend the ladder. Head north, then east, and walk onto the warp pad. You'll see another warp pad on the southwest corner of this platform that you will walk onto. Walk south and jump three ramps heading west to land on a platform with two warp pads. Walk onto the eastern one to arrive on a platform with a Max Elixer, use the warp pad to return to the previous spot. This time take the western warp pad and jump one ramp heading west to find a Big Nugget. Jump the ramp heading east and return to the previous spot via the warp pad. Walk one spot south, jump three ramps heading east, and then head north and walk onto the warp pad. On the platform you arrive on, walk onto the warp pad to the northeast. Walk up one step and hop two ramps heading east to find a Max Revive. Head south, jump four larger ramps, walk west and then onto the warp pad to the south. Walk south onto another warp pad, you'll find a Full Restore on this platform. Continue west onto the warp pad, north, and then east onto another warp pad. Finally, you'll ascend the ladder you see to the west which leads to the roof. After a short cutscene, Ho-oh will descend from the sky. Be sure and save before interacting with it! It will either be Lv 50 if you chose Gold, or Lv 70 if you chose Silver. If you catch it, return to the Ho-oh Puzzle Room with Ho-oh at the lead of your party to collect the items from the final hidden room. You can Fly from the the roof for a quick escape. It's time to head towards the Pokémon League!

Ho-oh Puzzle Room

Dodrio — 5% (Lv 42)		Pineco — 60% (Lv 10)
Exeggcute — 30% (Lv 10)

Fly to New Bark Town and surf on the water to the east. After surfing east a short distance, disembark onto the land to the south, and then enter the entrance to Tohjo Falls to the north. Inside the cave, surf onto the water and climb the waterfall to the north. Surf east and then go down the waterfall to the south. Disembark onto the land to the east and take the exit out of the cave. Note: disembarking on the east bank will lead to a room with a dead radio. This is a location where you can trigger an optional battle, however, due to the level of the trainer's Pokémon, we will be revisiting this location later on. If you'd like to give it a try now, click the link below to jump to that section of this walkthrough.

Radio Battle Event Link

As you exit Tohjo Falls, you'll get a phone call from Warden Baoba telling you that the Safari Zone has expanded. Walk south to battle Cool Trainer Megan who has a Lv 44 Tangela, a Lv 44 Venusaur, a Lv 44 Victreebel, and a Lv 44 Bellossom. Enter the house just east of Megan and talk to the NPC, if your follower has max friendship she will give you TM37 (Sandstorm). Surf eastward and disembark onto a bank with grass and a trainer. That trainer is Cool Trainer Brian, he has a Lv 45 Sandslash, a Lv 45 Sneasel, a Lv 44 Scizor, and a Lv 43 Ursaring. Head north to find a Moon Stone in the grass. Head east and then surf south onto the water just before the boardwalk. You'll see some land with a trainer and an item, disembark here. The trainer is Bird Keeper Jose, he has a Lv 45 Fearow, a Lv 45 Farfetch'd, a Lv 45 Pidgeot, and a Lv 45 Dodrio. The item on the ground is a Rare Candy. Surf north, disembark of the boardwalk, and head east to battle Cool Trainer Blake who has a Lv 43 Magneton, a Lv 44 Piloswine, a Lv 43 Exeggutor, and a Lv 44 Quagsire. Surf north to find a Revive on a small patch of ground, and then surf back to the boardwalk. Head east and then north up some stairs to battle with Psychic GIlbert who has a Lv 42 Xatu, a Lv 44 Girafarig, and a Lv 44 Exeggutor. Head east, north up a small set of stairs, east, north up a larger set of stairs, and then east to battle Cool Trainer Reena. She has a Lv 44 Starmie, a Lv 44 Vileplume, a Lv 43 Nidoqueen, a Lv 43 Electrode, and a Lv 45 Starmie. Head south down the first set of stairs and then east into some grass to find TM22 (Solarbeam). Head back west, south down a small set of stairs, east, south down a larger set of stairs, and then start heading east on the boardwalk. You'll see Fisherman Scott on the boardwalk, he has a Lv 45 Quilfish, a Lv 44 Seaking, a Lv 43 Gyarados, and a Lv 43 Quagsire. Continue east, north up a set of stairs, and then east to reach Route 26.

As you enter Route 26, continue east across a bridge, north across another bridge, east, and then north. You'll see a dead tree, to the west of this tree is Psychic Richard who has a Lv 45 Espeon, a Lv 45 Mr. Mime, and a Lv 45 Slowking. Head back east until you hit the treeline, and then head north past a small ledge through some grass and onto a dirt path. Continue north towards the house up ahead, you'll battle Cool Trainer Gaven who has a Lv 50 Victreebel, a Lv 50 Flareon, a Lv 50 Dragonaire, a Lv 50 Kingler, and a Lv 50 Donphan. Head inside the house and talk to the NPC to have your Pokémon healed. Exit the house and grab 5 Sitrus Berries from the berry tree just northwest of the house. Head north up a small set of stairs to the west,east, and north up another small set of stairs to battle Cool Trainer Beth. She has a Lv 45 Rapidash, a Lv 45 Lanturn, a Lv 45 Gengar, a Lv 45 Ampharos, and a Lv 45 Miltank. Head west and then north up a small set of stairs to battle Cool Trainer Jake who has a Lv 45 Cloyster, a Lv 47 Pidgeot, a Lv 45 Jolteon, a Lv 45 Arcanine, and a Lv 45 Alakazam. Head north up two small sets of stairs and then head west to battle Cool Trainer Joyce, she has a Lv 44 Blastoise, a Lv 45 Jumpluff, a Lv 46 Raichu, a Lv 45 Tauros, and a Lv 45 Rhydon. Finally, head north into the gate up ahead which leads to Victory Road.

As you head north through the gate, a guard will stop you to check if you have all 8 badges. Continue north through the large opening to enter Victory Road. Upon entering, head north and push the boulder up ahed from it's south side. Head east, south down a set of stairs, north, east across the top of cliff, past the large set of stairs to the north, into the eastern wall, and then north to find a Full Heal. Head back south, west, and take the large stairs to the north. Continue north across and bridge, take the stairs on the east side of the bridge south to find a Potion. Head north back up those stairs, west, and take the stairs on the west side of the stairs south. Head west into the western wall, north, west, and push the southern boulder two spaces west. You'll find a Max Revive. Head back east past the boulder we move d and head north up a large set up stairs and ascend the ladder in the northwest corner of this floor. On the next floor, head south from the ladder, east, and then north up two small sets of stairs to find an Hp Up. Head west and then north up another small set of stairs, east, south, east, and then south down a bigger set of stairs next to a ladder. Head east into the wall to the east, north, and then push the boulder sitting in front of some stairs all the way to the west and then go north to find a Full Restore. Head back to the three stairsets to the east and take the northern stairs, followed by another stairset to the north, and ascend the ladder in the northeast corner of this room. On the next floor, head east into some rocks and then south, avoiding the hole in the ground. Go west, north up the middle of a set of stairs, and continue north into a breakable rock. Break the two rocks here to find a Max Ether. Head back south, west across the cliff face perpendicular to the stairs to the south, and then go south while hugging the corner of that cliff. You'll see a hole in the ground to the south, ignore this, head west, and fall down the hole to the west. After falling through the hole, you'll find a Max Revive. Jump the ledge to the south and ascend the ladder. On the final floor, head north, west, north, and east around a cliff to find some breakable rocks to the south. Break three rocks to find a Rare Candy. Walk north into a wall, east, and then north into the partial enclosure with a hole in the ground to find an Ultra Ball. Head south back out of this partial enclosure, east past the area we previously broke the rocks, and then north. As you approach the exit, you'll be stopped by your Rival who will challenge you to another battle.

After defeating your Rival, head north and out the exit of Victory Road!

Indigo Plateau

Make your way north from the entrance/exit to Victory Road to enter the Pokémon League HQ. There is both a mart and a nurse inside this building. There is also an NPC with an Abra who will offer to teleport you home if you want more training. Whenever you are ready, be sure and stock up on items and heal up, then head to the northeast corner of the room and enter the the opening to take on the Elite 4!

Johto Elite 4

After defeating Lance, your team will be recorded into the Hall of Fame. If you are active in the Discord, we would love to have your team shared in the server's Hall of Fame! Once the credits have rolled and you are ready for the second half of the game, select Continue from the main menu, and awaken back in New Bark Town.

New Bark Town/Elm (S.S. Ticket)

As you awaken back in New Bark Town, you'll get a phone call from Elm asking you to stop by his lab. Head there and talk to Elm to be given the S.S. Ticket. This allows us to sail to Kanto from Olivine City. That will be our next destination.

Olivine City Port/National Dex

Fly to Olivine and head south from the Pokécenter towards the port. After walking a short distance south on the boardwalk, you'll arrive at the port itself. Inside, you'll see Professor Oak in front of a sailor, speak to him to have your Pokedex upgraded to National Mode, meaning more Pokémon can be registered into your dex. Speak to the sailor to the south to board the S.S. Aqua.

S.S. Aqua

As you enter the boat, you will be stopped by an old man looking for his grandaughter, cluing us in to our next objective: finding her. You'll notice this floor is seperated into a north and south hallway, each with four rooms. Start first by heading to the western room in the northern hallway. In here you will find a PC and a bed where you can heal your Pokémon. Heading east, in the next room you'll battle Sailor Stanly who has a Lv 53 Quilfish, a Lv 53 Machoke, and a Lv 54 Golduck. After battle he will run off to rejoin his crew. Note: this is the only trainer you need to battle in order to progress the story on the ship. Exit and reenter the same room to find two new trainers. Burglar Corey has two Lv 55 Koffing, a Lv 58 Magmar, and a Lv 60 Koffing. Gentleman Edward has a Lv 63 Persian. Exit this room and head to the next one to the east to have a double battle with Hiker Noland & Pokemaniac Ethan, they have a Lv 55 Sandslash, a Lv 61 Haunter, a Lv 55 Golem, and a Lv 61 Rhydon. Exit this room and enter the last room in this northern hallway to the east. Inside you'll battle Cool Trainer Sean who has a Lv 60 Flareon, a Lv 60 Tangela, and a Lv 60 Tauros. The other NPC is Cool Trainer Carol who has a Lv 60 Electrode, a Lv 60 Ninetales, and a Lv 61 Starmie. Exit this room and walk around the east side, past a set of stairs, and enter the easternmost cabin in the southern hallway. In side you'll find the NPC who's grandchild we are searching more. Enter the next cabin to the west to battle Pokefan Colin who has a Lv 56 Delibird. Twins Meg & Peg are also in here, they have a Lv 54 Ursaring and a Lv 54 Donphan. In the next cabin to the west you'll battle Guitarist Clyde who has a Lv 61 Electabuzz. You can also battle Beauty Cassie who has a Lv 60 Vileplume and a Lv 62 Jynx. In the westernmost cabin of the southern hallway you'll battle Firebreather Lyle who has a Lv 54 Weezing, a Lv 54 Flareon, and a Lv 54 Ninetales. You can also battle Bug Catcher Ken who has a Lv 60 Ariados and a Lv 62 Pinsir. That's all the trainers for this floor, head back to the first cabin to heal up if necessary, then descend the stairs in the southeast corner of this floor. On the lower deck, head north, west, and then south into s semi-enclosure with some beds. Talk to Juggler Fritz by the trashcan, he has a Lv 54 Mr. Mime, a Lv 54 Magmar, and a Lv 56 Machoke. In the northwest corner of this enclosure is Picnicker Debra who has a Lv 58 Seaking. By a bed is Fisherman Jonah who has a Lv 55 Shellder, a Lv 59 Octillery, a Lv 55 Remoraid, and a Lv 59 Cloyster. Head north out of this area and then west along the northern hallway. You can battle two sailors running around, the first being Sailor Garret who has a Lv 64 Kingler. The second is Sailor Kenneth who has a Lv 58 Machop, a Lv 58 Machoke, a Lv 58 Poliwrath, and a Lv 58 Machamp. Enter the second semi-enclosure to see four NPCs sitting at a couple of tables. Starting with the northwestern trainer, you'll battle Expert Shirley who has a Lv 61 Jigglypuff and a Lv 63 Wigglytuff. The trainer below her is School Kid Ricky who has a Lv 62 Aipom and a Lv 62 Ditto. Next up is School Kid Nate who has a Lv 62 Ledian and a Lv 62 Exeggutor. The final trainer is Black Belt Wai who has a Lv 56 Machamp, a Lv 58 Machoke, and a Lv 60 Machamp. Exit this semi enclosed room and ascend the stairs to the southwest. You can battle Sailor Jeff here, he has a Lv 58 Raticate and a Lv 58 Furret. Enter the room ahead to find the little girl talking to the captain. Speak with her to take her back to her grandfather who will award you a Metal Coat. As you exit his room, you'll be notified that the S.S. Aqua has reached Viridian City! Head north and speak to the sailor to exit the boat onto the continent of Kanto!

Truck of Myth (Old Sea Map)/Suicune Sighting 3

You'll exit Vermilion CIty Port onto a boardwalk, take the eastern path, surf north, and then surf east onto a patch of land in the middle of the bay. You'll find the Old Sea Map behind a truck here. Surf west back onto the boardwalk and follow the path north. As you progress, you'll eventually see a cutscene with Suicune and Eusine. To the west is Vermilion City proper.

Vermilion City

Encounter List LandRock Smash/HeadbuttSurfOld RodGood RodSuper Rod
Geodude — 95% (Lv 20-22)

Welcome to Vermilion City! To the north is Route 6, the path east is currently blocked by a Snorlax. From the Pokécenter, follow the road east, south, and then onto the first road going west. The second building here is the Pokémon Fan Club. Speak to the older NPC on the northwest edge of the table and respond to his question with "Yes" to be given a Rare Candy. The only thing left to collect in Vermilion is our badge, however, let's check out a few new areas that are now unlocked, including seeing where that Old Sea Map takes us!

Trainer Hill

From the Pokécenter in Olivine, head west out of town towards Route 40. At the northwest corner of the route is a gate to the north. Inside, you'll find a move tutor who can teach your Pokémon Substitute. Continue north through the gate to reach Trainer Hill. There are multiple buildings here that provide unique challenges. As you enter Trainer Hill, keep north at the first branch in the path to head up two sets of stairs to reach a large building. This is the Trainer Hill. Just south of the Trainer Hill are three Move Tutors that will teach you Pokémon moves in exchange for BP. The male NPC has the following moves to offer: Frenzy Plant- 64BP Blast Burn- 64BP Hydro Cannon- 64BP The eastern woman NPC has the following moves to offer: Defense Curl- 16BP Snore- 24 BP Mud-Slap- 24BP Swift- 24BP Icy Wind- 24BP Endure- 48BP Psych Up- 48BP Ice Punch- 48BP Thunder Punch- 48BP Fire Punch- 48BP The western woman NPC has the following moves to offer: Softboiled- 16BP Seismic Toss- 24BP Dream Eater- 24BP Mega Punch- 24BP Mega Kick- 48BP Body Slam- 48BP Rock Slide- 48BP Counter- 48BP Thunder Wave- 48BP Swords Dance- 48BP Back to the Trainer Hill building, here, you can take on a challenge called Time Attack. It is a race to the top of the building; the faster you finish, the better the prize. Each floor will feature a double battle, all Pokémon will be Lv 50, including those of yours that are either lower or higher leveled. Your Pokémon will revert back to their actual levels once the challenge is finished. I will update this section once I've done more testing to reflect the potential prizes you can win. Note: The Trainer Hill will be locked until you have beaten the Johto E4 Back outside, head south, and then east at the first branch. You'll see two NPCs standing next to a sign, both are merchants which will exchange items for BP. The western merchant sells vitamins for 1BP a piece. The eastern merchant has the following items to offer: Leftovers- 48BP White Herb-48BP Quick Claw- 48BP Mental Herb- 48BP Brightpowder- 64BP Choice Band- 64BP King's Rock- 64BP Focus Band- 64BP Scope Lens- 64BP Continuing east, there are three Battle Tents which each provide unique challenges. The easternmost tent is the Battle Tent: Palace. Here, you enter pick three Pokémon and can only choose to switch them out in battle--the Pokémon themselves are left to select their moves. Defeating three trainers in a row will be awarded with various prizes. The northern tent is the Battle Tent: Arena. Here, you can take on a the Set-KO Tourney. You'll select three Pokémon and the order you set them in will be the order they are deployed in battle. Once a Pokémon enters battle, you cannot switch it out until the match ends, or it is knocked out. Defeating three trainers in a row will be awarded with various prizes. The eastern tent is the Battle Tent: Factory. Here, you'll use Rental Pokémon to take on Battle Swap events. You'll choose three rental Pokémon to start with, and will have the option to swap one of those Pokémon for another from the Pokémon used by a trainer you defeat. Defeating three trainers in a row will be awarded with various prizes. Once again, I will update this section to reflect the potential prizes once I have gotten more time to test it out. Till then, have fun taking on these unique challenges!

Battle Frontier

Once you have arrived at Vermilion City, you can now sail to the Battle Frontier from Olivine City. Note: Once you've restored power to the Power Plant, you'll be able to set sail from Vermilion City as well. Speak to the sailor at either city's docks, and respond with "Battle Frontier" to be taken there. Upon arrival, enter the building you see to the east. You'll be given a Frontier Pass by an attendant as you enter. You will also be introduced to an NPC named Scott. Our first objective will be to locate the Pokécenter, which will be the spot from which I'll be directing you around the Frontier. After exiting the first building, walk thirteen steps north, and then head east until you see a wooden bridge that leads east. Cross this bridge to arrive just west of the Pokécenter! Exchange Service: From the Pokécenter, start walking directly north. The Exchange Service is the building with a red roof. There are multiple vendors which exchange items for BP. The western merchant facing south has the following items to offer: Lapras Doll- 128BP Snorlax Doll- 128 BP Venusaur Doll- 256 BP Charizard Doll- 256 BP Blastoise Doll- 256 BP The eastern merfchant facing south offers Vitamins for 1 BP a piece. The merchant facing west has the following items to offer: Treecko Doll: 48 BP Torchic Doll: 48 BP Mudkip Doll: 48 BP Togepi Doll: 48 BP Meowth Doll: 48 BP Clefairy Doll: 48 BP Ditto Doll: 48 BP Cyndaquil Doll: 80 BP Chikorita Doll: 80 BP Totodile Doll: 80 BP The merchant facing east has the following items to offer: Leftovers- 48BP White Herb-48BP Quick Claw- 48BP Mental Herb- 48BP Brightpowder- 64BP Choice Band- 64BP King's Rock- 64BP Focus Band- 64BP Scope Lens- 64BP Ranking Hall: From the Pokécenter, take two steps west, four steps north, twelve steps east, north until you run into a cliff, east until you run into a cliff, north until you run into a cliff, one step east, one step north, eight steps east, then walk north, the building you come to is the Ranking Hall. In here, your records will be posted from the various competitions within the Battle Frontier. Frontier Brains: Each building have a unique Frontier Brain which essentially act as boss battles within their respective competitions. These trainers show up as you get longer streaks. Battle Tower: From the Pokécenter, take two steps west, four steps north, four steps east, and then walk north, the massive building you arrive at is the Battle Tower. Here, you participate in battles using the normal battle rules. Each round consists of seven battles. Winning all seven will be rewarded with BP. The longer your streak, the more BP is awarded. You can either sign up for Single, Double, or Multi Battles. Single and Double battles are basic battles. With Multi Battles, you are paired with an NPC and battle as a team, you don't have control over your teammates Pokémon. The Frontier Brain of the Battle Tower is Anabel, she will appear in Single Battles after reaching a streak of 34 and again after 69. Battle Dome: From the Pokécenter, head across the bridge just east of the Pokécenter. In the next area, walk ten steps west, four steps north, fourteen steps west, and then walk north until you see a large building. This is the Battle Dome. In the battle dome, you will bring three Pokémon along with you. One of your Pokémon will be kept in reserve. You'll get to examine your opponents team beforehand, and then select which two Pokémon you wish to battle with. You win the tournament by defeating four trainers, the amount of BP awarded increases the longer your streak. You can choose to either enter the Single Battles tournament or Double Battles tournament. There is a bridge to the northeast of the Battle Dome. Just west of this bridge is a small house. Inside you will find Scott, who will give you 1 BP. The Frontier Brain of the Battle Dome is Dome Ace Tucker, he will appear after winning four consecutive tournaments, and again after winning nine consecutive tournaments. Battle Palace: From the Pokécenter, take one step south, sixteen steps east, ten steps south, and then head east to find a fancy looking red building. This is the Battle Palace. Here, battles play out as usual, except that the only thing you can do as a trainer is to switch your Pokémon in/out. The Pokémon themselves decide which moves to use. Winning seven battles in a row will be rewarded with BP, the amount of BP won is increased the longer your streak. You can choose to enter Single Battles or Double Battles. The Frontier Brain of the Battle Palace is Palace Maven Spencer, he will battle you after winning twenty consecutive battles, and again after winning forty-one consecutive battles. Battle Arena: From the Pokécenter, take two steps west, four steps north, twenty-nine steps east, then head north to arrive at a building with 6 columns and a black roof. This is the Battle Arena. Here, you will participate in the Set KO Tournament. You select three Pokémon to participate in battles that take up three turns. You cannot switch out Pokémon. When the three turns are up, if you haven't knocked out all of your opponents Pokémon, the participants will be judged according to how well you battled. The amount of hits landed, offensive moves used, and the amount of HP left all factor into how the battle is judged. Winning seven battles in a row will be rewarded with BP, the longer your streak, the more BP is awarded. The Frontier Brain of the Battle Arena is Greta, you'll face her after winning twenty-seven consecutive battles, and again after winning fifty-five consecutive battles. Battle Factory: From the Pokécenter, head west across the bridge. In the next area, take ten steps west, two steps south, twenty-nine steps west, and then head north to find a large yellow and grey striped building. This is the Battle Factory. Here, you will participate in Battle Swap Tournaments. You will participate with Rental Pokémon, you'll be initially given a pool of six Pokémon from which you will create your party of three. Between battles, you can swap one of your Pokémon for one used by the opponent you just beat. Winning seven consecutive battles will net you BP, the longer your streak, the more BP awarded. The Frontier Brain is Noland, he will challenge you after winning twenty consecutive battles, and again after winning forty-one consecutive battles. Battle Pike: From the Pokécenter, head west across the bridge. In the next area, take eight steps west, then head north to find a building shaped like a Seviper. This is the Battle Pike. Here, you will take place in a competition called Battle Choice. Each run consists of fourteen rooms. When you start, you'll be given three different paths from which you'll choose one to take. There will be a maid which gives you a hint about what might lie in wait in one of the rooms. The potential hints are as follows: "I must have heard something.... It may have been whispering" - This means that you will either participate in a Double Battle, or there might just be an NPC talking to themselves. "Is it... a Trainer? I sense the presence of people" - This means you will either battle a trainer or find a maid who will heal your Pokémon. "For some odd reason, I felt a wave of nostalgia coming from it" - This means you'll either run into a man who's Pokémon is running around which will inflict status conditions on your Pokémon or a man who will heal one or two of your Pokémon. "It has the distinct aroma of Pokémon wafting around it" - This means the room will either have wild Pokémon or you will battle a trainer. A maid will heal your Pokémon if you battle a trainer here. The Frontier Brain is Queen Lucy, she will show up in the twenty-eighth room and once again in the 140th room. Battle Pyramid: From the Pokécenter, take two steps west, four steps north, forty-eight steps east, and then north to find a large building shaped like a pyramid. Unsurprisingly, this is the Battle Pyramid. Here, your aim to navigate through seven floors filled with wild Pokémon. The floors are poorly lit, and you are looking to for warp pad which take you to the next floor. Your bag is taken from you beforehand and will be replaced with a Battle Bag. Any items you find in the Pyramid will remain in your Battle Bag until they are either used, or you lose. To be clear, items will carry over to the next challenge if you reach the top of the pyramid. The Frontier Brain is Brandon, he'll meet you on the top of the Pyramid on your third and fifth attempts.

Faraway Island/Mew

Encounter List LandRock Smash/HeadbuttSurfOld RodGood RodSuper Rod
Pichu — 20% (Lv 5)
Wynaut — 20% (Lv 5)
Elekid — 10% (Lv 5)
Magby — 10% (Lv 5)
Mudkip — 10% (Lv 5)
Torchic — 10% (Lv 5)
Treecko — 10% (Lv 5)
Azurill — 5% (Lv 5)
Smoochum — 5% (Lv 5)

Once the Old Sea Map is acquired, this section becomes available. Fly to Olivine, speak to the sailor at the docks, and respond with "Faraway Island". You'll be taken there. Head north from the small pier onto the island itself. The path to the west leads to a faded sign. Head north instead, up a small set of stairs, west, south, west, north up two more sets of stairs and into some grass. You'll have some unique encounters here. Follow the grass north and then east around the trees in the center, continuing east across a small pond, and then north into another area. Here, you'll see Mew spot you before hiding in some taller grass. Mew will mirror your movements while in the grass. I don't have any great hints for how to do it, just enter the tall grass and press A whenever you move, the goal is to interact with Mew's sprite in the overworld. Eventually, you will find a Lv 50 Mew. This is one of the legendaries that you can't run from and then restart the encounter, so be sure and save beforehand! There's nothing else to do on this island aside from catching Pokémon, head back to the sailor once you are finished and speak to him to leave the island.

Vermilion City Gym

Fly back to Vermilion City and heal up, it's time to earn our first badge in Kanto! From the Pokécenter, head east, south, then west onto a road. Cut a tree and enter the gym on the southern side of the road. Before worrying about the gym's gimmick, let's take on the trainers. First up is Guitarist Vincent who has two Lv 57 Magnetons, a Lv 55 Electrode, and a Lv 55 Jolteon. On the east side of this room is Juggler Horton who has two Lv 56 Electrodes, a Lv 57 Electrode, and a Lv 58 Electrode. The final trainer is Gentleman Gregory who has a Lv 57 Pikachu and a Lv 56 Ampharos. To access Lt Surge, you have to find two electronic switches in the trashcans. The first switch is completely random, the second should be in one of the trashcans close to the first switch. Picking the wrong trashcan after finding the first switch resets the puzzle. Soft reseting after finding the first switch also resets the puzzle. If you can use save states, you can save a state right after finding the first switch and reset from there to easily find the second switch. Once both switches are found, the locks will remain permanently disengaged. Head north to battle Leader LtSurge, he has a Lv 57 Electrode holding a Focus Band, a Lv 59 Magnezone, a Lv 58 Lanturn holding Leftovers, a Lv 58 Magnetric, a Lv 59 Electivire holding a Scope Lens, and a Lv 60 Raichu holding a Magnet. Upon victory you'll be awarded the Thunderbadge which increases your Pokémon's speed. Surge will also give you TM24 (Thunderbolt). Be sure and heal up at the Pokécenter and then head north out of Vermilion towards Route 6!

Route 6/Warden Baoba Call

Follow the dirt path north to battle PokeFan Allan, he has a Lv 55 Granbull. Continue following the path east, north, west, and then north to battle PokeFan Rex who has a Lv 55 Stantler. Continue north through two gaps in ledges to find a berry tree which yields 10 Grepa Berries. As you enter the gate to the north, you'll receive another phone call from Warden Baoba informing you that the final area of the Johto Safari Zone has been finished. Continue through the gate to reach Saffron City!

Saffron City

As you first enter Saffron City from Route 6, you'll find the Pokécenter on the west side of the first road going east to west. Saffron City is made up of four streets going east to west and two streets going north to south. Aside from the Pokécenter, there is one other house of interest to enter on the southernmost street. To the east of the Pokécenter, enter the house and speak to an NPC to be given TM29 (Psychic). We will be heading to SIlph Co, the large building in the center of town.

Silph Co. Steven (Hoenn Starter)

From the Pokécenter in Saffron, head west to the street going north/south, north, and then east onto the first street going east/west. Enter the large building in the middle of this street. The NPC in front of the fountain in this room is Steven, the Hoenn Champion. Speak to him to be asked to pick a colored stone, your choice will dictate which Pokémon he gifts you. "Green Stone" will net a Lv 5 Treecko, "Red Stone" will net a Lv 5 Torchic, and "Blue Stone" will net a Lv 5 Mudkip. Head to the northeast corner of this room and speak to the guard in front of some stairs to be given an Up-Grade. That's it for SIlph Co!

Fighting Dojo

On the northermost road going east-west you'll find two buildings with gym signage. The western of these two buildings is the Fighting Dojo, the eastern building is the Saffron City Gym. Head into the Fighting Dojo. This first room is essentially an EV training arena. You can interact with any of the Pokémon to battle a Lv 1 encounter of their respective species, each of which yields 3EVs of a specific stat. The NPC to east is the Ability Expert, he can swap a Pokémon's ability for ₽25,000. Note: Not every Pokémon has multiple abilities. You can talk to the nurse to heal your party. Continue into the next room to the north. This next room will be relatively barren if you've been following the walkthrough up to this point. We will start encountering gym leaders in various places in the overworld after you've beaten them. Talking to the leaders will lead them to being transported to this room, where they will be available to battle again with tougher teams. Beating gym leaders in these rematches will yield 10BP. BP can be used at various vendors in this room. First, you'll notice another Nurse at the center counter on the north side of the room, you can heal up here. The NPC at the counter east of the Nurse will trade these items for BP: Leftovers- 48BP White Herb-48BP Quick Claw- 48BP Mental Herb- 48BP Brightpowder- 64BP Choice Band- 64BP King's Rock- 64BP Focus Band- 64BP Scope Lens- 64BP The NPC at the counter west of the Nurse sells vitamins for 1BP a piece. You'll see a table just south of the western counter with three NPCS around it--these are the Move Tutors. The woman NPC on the north side of the table has the following moves to offer: Defense Curl- 16BP Snore- 24 BP Mud-Slap- 24BP Swift- 24BP Icy Wind- 24BP Endure- 48BP Psych Up- 48BP Ice Punch- 48BP Thunderpunch- 48BP Fire Punch- 48BP The male NPC sitting on the northwest chair at this table has the following moves to offer: Frenzy Plant- 64BP Blast Burn- 64BP Hydro Cannon- 64BP The NPC sitting on the southwest chair at this table has the following moves to offer: Softboiled- 16BP Seismic Toss- 24BP Dream Eater- 24BP Mega Punch- 24BP Mega Kick- 48BP Body Slam- 48BP Rock Slide- 48BP Counter- 48BP Thunder Wave- 48BP Swords Dance- 48BP That's it for the Fighting Dojo for now, I will be pointing out where we find the Gym Leaders as we naturally explore and will also have a section summarizing their locations and teams towards the end of this walkthrough. Be sure you are healed up and head to the building next door, it's time to take on the Saffron City Gym!

Saffron City Gym

This gym requires navigating a warp pads to find yourself to the gym leader Sabrina. Take the warp in the first room. You can battle Psychic Jared in the next room, he has a Lv 58 Unown, a Lv 58 Exeggutor, and a Lv 58 Mr. Mime. Take the warp pad on the southwest corner to the next room. Here, you can battle Hex Maniac Rebecca who has two Lv 58 Hypnos, and a Lv 58 Jynx. Take the warp pad on the northwest corner to the next room. Here, you can battle Psychic Doris who has a Lv 58 Noctowl, a Lv 59 Slowbro, and a Lv 58 Xatu.Take the warp pad on the southwest corner to the next room. Here, you can battle Psychic Franklin who has a Lv 60 Alakazam. Take the warp pad on the southwest corner to arrive at the final room where you can battle Leader Sabrina. Sabrina has a Lv 65 Mr. Mime holding a Quick Claw, a Lv 64 Jynx, a Lv 64 Slowbro holding a Chesto Berry, a Lv 64 Wobbuffet holding Leftovers, a Lv 65 Espeon, and a Lv 66 Alakazam holding a Twistedspoon. You'll be awarded the Marshbadge upon victory!

Take the gate heading west out of Saffron City to arrive at Route 7. To the south is a berry tree which yields 10 Tamato Berries. The only other thing of interest here is the patch of grass to the north. Head west to arrive at Celadon City.

Celadon City

Encounter List LandRock Smash/HeadbuttSurfOld RodGood RodSuper Rod
Pineco — 60% (Lv 10)
Exeggcute — 30% (Lv 10)
Ledyba — 5% (Lv 10)
Spinarak — 5% (Lv 10)		Grimer — 99% (Lv 15-24)
Muk — 1% (Lv 15-19)		Grimer — 100% (Lv 5)Grimer — 100% (Lv 5)Grimer — 100% (Lv 5)

Welcome to Celadon City! As you enter town from Route 7, you'll see a Pokécenter to the north. From the Pokécenter, head west on the white path and then south on a grassy path between two buildings towards another white path. The larger of the two buildings on the west side of this grassy path is the Celadon Game Corner. Follow the white path west, south at the first fork, and then east across the lower row of buildings. You'll eventually find a berry tree that yields 10 Hondew Berries. Head back west from this berry tree, and then head north into the first building that you can enter. This is the Celadon Diner. Interact with the trash can in the southeast corner of the room to find Leftovers. Exit the building and head back to the Pokécenter.

Celadon City Mansion

From the Pokécenter in Celadon, take the grassy path on the east side of the Pokécenter north, west, north, west, and then south into a back entrance to the Celadon Mansion. Ascending three sets of stairs will land you on the roof of the building. Here, you'll see a smaller building to the south of the staircase. Enter and speak to the NPC during nighttime to get TM44 (Rest). This clears the backside of the Mansion. Head back to the Pokécenter and, this time, head west and then north to the front entrance of the Mansion. Ascend the staircase to the second floor. Here, you'll meet NPCs representative of multiple Devs/Playtesters of H&S! Ascend another staircase to arrive at the third floor. Here, you'll meet NPCs representative of the Gamefreak Dev Team. If you talk to the NPC with blue hair after catching every Pokémon, you'll be awarded a Diploma. That's it for this building. Make your way back to the first floor and exit.

Celadon City Dept Store

From the Celadon Mansion, head west to find a large building. This is the Celadon Dept Store. On the third floor, you'll find the TM shop. Here you can buy TM38 (Fire Blast), TM25 (Thunder), TM14 (Blizzard), and TM15 (Hyper Beam). On the fifth floor, you can find Leader Falkner. Speak to him here to transport him to the Saffron Fighting Dojo. You can now challenge him to a rematch whenever you'd like! That's the last of the unique things to find here. Exit the store and heal up at the Pokécenter. It's time to take on the Celadon City Gym!

Celadon City Gym

From the Celadon Pokécenter, head west and then south down the grassy path between two buildings. Once again, follow the white path west, south at the first fork, and then east along the southern row of houses. South of the Celadon Diner is a tree. After cutting the tree down, follow the path west and then north to arrive at the Celadon City Gym! After entering the gym, cut a tree and walk north to battle Twins Jo & Zoe who have a Lv 58 Victreebel and a Lv 58 Vileplume. Up ahead, the trainer to the east is Beauty Julia, she has a Lv 58 Parasect, a Lv 58 Sunflora, and a Lv 56 Exeggutor. Across from Julia to the west is Picnicker Tanya who has a Lv 57 Bellossom, a Lv 57 Sunflora, and a Lv 57 Exeggutor. Continue north to battle Lass Michelle, she has two Lv 57 Jumpluffs and a Lv 58 Jumpluff. All that is left is to battle Leader Erika, she has a Lv 61 Jumpluff holding a Focus Band, a Lv 60 Roserade, a Lv 60 Tangrowth, a Lv 61 Venusaur holding Leftovers, a Lv 62 Victreebel holding a Poison Barb, and a Lv 62 Bellossom holding a Miracle Seed. Winning the battle will net you the Rainbowbadge, as well as TM19 (Giga Drain). That's it for the gym! Exit the building and heal up at the Pokécenter. There is one final thing to do in Celadon as we make our way out of town. From the Pokécenter, head west to the fountain in front of the Celadon Dept Store. You'll see Leader Erika here, speak to her to have her transported to the Saffron City Fighting Dojo where you can now have rematches with her. Continue west out of town to arrive at Route 16.

Route 17

Route 17, or Cycling Road, requires you to be on a bike throughout. For a large portion of the route, you will automatically go south due to an incline. As you enter the route, head west to the end of the dirt path, then head south. You'll see two grey roads, take the east one for now. You'll run into Biker Joel who has a Lv 58 Magmar and a Lv 58 Electabuzz. Head back north to the top of the route and then take the western road south. First, you'll battle Biker Riley, he has a Lv 60 Weezing. Continue south to eventually reach Biker Charles, he has a Lv 56 Poliwrath, a Lv 56 Weezing, and a Lv 56 Charizard. Continue south down the path into a fence, from there, head east onto the road to the east. Head north up this road to battle the final trainer, Biker Glenn. He has a Lv 57 Koffing, a Lv 55 Magmar, and a Lv 58 Weezing. After battling Glenn, head south as far as the route will let you, and then head east through a gate to exit the route.

As soon as you exit the gate from Route 17, head south into a patch of grass. The first trainer is Bird Keeper Boris who has a Lv 60 Yanma, a Lv 58 Murkrow, and a Lv 60 Dodrio. The other trainer is Bird Keeper Bob who has a Lv 61 Noctowl. Head east from here to reach Fuchsia City!

Fuchsia City

Encounter List LandRock Smash/HeadbuttSurfOld RodGood RodSuper Rod
Pineco — 60% (Lv 10)

As you enter Fuchsia City from Route 18, follow the brick road east. Cut a tree to the north to access a berry tree next to a pond with a Kabuto in it, this berry tree yields 5 Sitrus Berries. Return to the brick path and follow it north past the first branch heading east and then heading west at the second branch in the path. Follow this path past the Pokémart to reach another berry tree which yields 10 Qualot Berries. That's all to collect in the city. From the Qualot tree, follow the brick patch east, south at the branch. You'll see the red roof of the Pokécenter to the south, walk through the grassy path southward to find your way to the Pokécenter. Next, we will want to head to the gate on the east side of town to trigger a phone call before heading to the Safari Zone on the north side of town. From the Pokécenter, jump the ledge to the east and take the grassy path north back to the brick road. Follow this road east into the gate on Route 15. You'll get a phone call from Warden Baoba saying that he heard the Fuchsia City Safari Zone reopened. He will ask you to catch one of the exotic Pokémon found there and bring it back to him at the Johto Safari Zone. Exit the gate and follow the brick path back west. Go north at the first branch of the path to reach the Kanto Safari Zone!

Kanto Safari Zone

Beach Area Encounter List LandRock Smash/HeadbuttSurfOld RodGood RodSuper Rod

As you enter the Kanto Safari Zone, Steven will introduce himself again as the owner of the Safari Zone. He'll also say that you can upgrade the Safari Zone at any time by talking to the attendant at the counter to the west. Each upgrade costs a substantial amount of money. However, by fulfilling Warden Baoba's request, you'll be rewarded with the money needed to upgrade the Safari Zone all the way, so we will focus on doing that first. You'll also see berry blending machines to the east of this room. Head north, pay the ₽500 fee, and enter the Safari Zone. Your access to other areas will be blocked off by guards. To fulfill Baoba's request, you must catch a Gen 3 Pokémon. All the wild Pokémon in this first area fit the bill so catch at least one Pokémon, and then once you've retired, add that Pokémon to your party and Fly to the Johto Safari Zone Gate. Head north into the Safari Zone gate and speak to Baoba at the table to the west, he will reward you with ₽200,000 and ask you to use it to upgrade the Kanto Safari Zone and bring him back a Pokémon from the Brush area. Fly back to Fuchsia City and head to the Safari Zone and speak to the southern attendant at the counter to unlock the Brush area.

Enter the Safari Zone and follow the dirt path north into a treeline. Head west from here to reach the Brush. After catching another gen 3 Pokémon from the Brush area, return to Baoba at the Johto Safari Zone. Showing him that Pokémon will net you ₽300,000 to be used to unlock the Mountain area in the Kanto Safari Zone. He will ask you to show him a Pokémon from that area once again. Fly back to Fuchsia City and speak to the attendent at the Safari Zone counter to unlock the Mountain area.

Fly back to Fuchsia City once you are ready to continue.

Fuchsia City Gym

From the Pokécenter, head west to find the Fuchsia City Gym. This gym has invisible walls to navigate around, along with trainers that disguise themselves as the Leader. To battle the first trainer, from the entrance of the gym take one step east, eight steps north, and then one step east and speak to the NPC. She will reveal herself to be Lass Alice who has two Lv 58 Vileplumes, and a Lv 59 Arbok. Return to the entrance of the gym. From here, walk six steps to the east, and then 13 steps north. Talking to the NPC will start a battle with Lass Linda, she has a Lv 58 Venusaur, a Lv 58 Quilfish, and a Lv 58 Muk. Walk five steps to the west and then 4 steps north. Speak to the NPC here to battle Picnicker Cindy, she has a Lv 57 Tentacruel and a Lv 60 Nidoqueen. Walk five steps to the west and then two steps south. Talk to the NPC here to battle Camper Berry, he has a Lv 57 Tentacruel and a Lv 60 Nidoking. Take one step south, two steps west, four steps south, and finally two steps east. It is finally time to battle Leader Janine, she has a Lv 63 Weezing holding Leftovers, a Lv 62 Muk, a Lv 61 Swalot, a Lv 62 Nidoqueen holding a Quick Claw, a Lv 63 Crobat, and a Lv 64 Venomoth holding a Bright Powder. Upon victory, you'll be given the Soulbadge and TM06 Toxic. To exit the gym, walk two steps west, four steps north, two steps east, three steps north, seven steps east, three steps south, four steps east, thirteen steps south, and then a final six steps west. Exit the gym and heal up, there are a bunch of trainers coming up on the next few routes.

Lavender Town

As you enter Lavender Town from Route 12 to the South, the first house you see to the west is the residence of another Name Rater. The large building in town is the Kanto Radio Tower. Speaking to NPC's in here will reveal that the Power Plant is experiencing issues, clueing us to our next objective. Heal up at the Pokécenter and head north out of town towards Route 10.

Route 10 South

As you enter Route 10 from Lavender Town to the south, head north, east, and then north through two gaps between ledges to battle Hiker Jim who has a Lv 58 Machamp. Go north one step, and then head west to battle Pokefan Robert who has a Lv 60 Kangaskhan. To the west you'll find an Ultra Ball. Enter the cave opening to the north to reach the Rock Tunnel.

Seaking — 4% (Lv 10-14)		Magikarp — 70% (Lv 10)

Magikarp — 44% (Lv 20-40)
Poliwhirl — 1% (Lv 40)

As you enter Cerulean City from Route 9 to the east, head west as you enter town to find the Pokécenter to the south. There aren't any NPC's in town with goodies to give, so let's follow up on that tip from the Power Plant guard, and head to the Gym to investigate.

Machine Part

As you enter the gym, a Rocket Grunt will run into you. He'll ask you to forget him, and run off. Leave the gym and follow the white path east, north, and then west. Align yourself with the roof of the Pokécenter and head north to reach Route 24. You'll see the Rocket Grunt on the bridge you are on, talk to him to start a battle, he has a Lv 30 Koffing. Upon victory, he'll tell you that he hid the Machine Part in the Cerulean City gym. Fly back to Cerulean and enter the gym. You'll notice a flash on the floor just below the central pool of water. Interact with the tile that flashed to find the Machine Part. Our next objective is to return this part to the Power Plant!

Power Plant Revisited/Zapdos

Along with fixing the generator, we will also have the opportunity to catch Zapdos at the Power Plant, so be sure and heal up and stock up on Pokeballs if necessary! From Cerulean City, take the path east out of town towards Route 9. Jump a ledge to the south, walk to the square east of the trainer you see, and head east from there. Walk until you run into a cliff, then head north through a gap between a ledge and that cliff. Continue north through a gap between two ledges, head east, jump down a ledge to the south, and continue east to reach Route 10. Head north through a gap in the fence and surf east, south, and then disembark on the coast to the west to reach the Power Plant once again. Enter the Power Plant and make your way to the opening in the wall towards the northeast corner of this room. Inside, speak to the Manager once more to return the Machine Part. You'll be given TM25 (Thunder) as a reward. Exit and then re-enter this room. Upon re-entry, you'll notice the worker guarding the exit to the west is no longer there. Take this exit to find a Lv 50 Zapdos!

Route 24

From Cerulean City, take the bridge north out of town to reach Route 24. Continue north as far as you can. You'll notice a berry tree behind some ledges, it yields 10 Kelpsy Berries. Jump the ledge south of this tree, and head east to reach Route 25.

Route 25

Continue east as you enter Route 25 to battle School Kid Dudley, he has a Lv 56 Vileplume. The next trainer is Lass Ellen who has a Lv 55 Wigglytuff and a Lv 55 Granbull. Walk to the square just north Ellen and walk north one step, three steps east, and two steps south to battle School Kid Joe who has a Lv 56 Tangela and a Lv 56 Vaporeon. Walk one step west, two steps north, one step west, three steps north, and then four steps east to battle Lass Laura. Laura has a Lv 55 Bellossom, a Lv 55 Politoed, and a Lv 55 Pidgeot. Walk south three steps, east two steps, and then interact with Camper Lloyd from behind, he has a Lv 57 Nidoking. Walk two steps west, three steps north, four steps east, and then three steps north to battle Lass Shannon. Shannon has a two Lv 56 Parasects, and a a Lv 55 Tangela. Walk south one step and head east. You'll see a berry tree which yields 10 Kelpsy Berries behind a cuttable tree. Just east is Super Nerd Pat who has a Lv 56 Porygon, a Lv 56 Magneton, and a Lv 56 Porygon2. Continue east to meet Cool Trainer Kevin who will award you with a Big Nugget for beating all the trainers, and then challenge you to a battle. Kevin has a Lv 58 Rhyhorn, a Lv 57 Lanturn, a Lv 58 Charizard, and a Lv 57 Espeon. Continue east, past a house to the north, to find a cliff with two NPCs on it. Approach the NPCs to cause one to run off, and the other to complain about your interruption. She will eventually reveal herself to be the Cerulean Gym Leader Misty, and tell you to come challenge her at here gym. Before we do this, head into the house we just walked by on this route.

Bill's Grandpa

Inside the house on Route 25 you'll meet Bill's grandpa. He will ask you to show him different Pokémon and will reward you for doing so. The first Pokémon he wants to see is Spinda, which can be found on Route 5. You'll be awarded an Everstone. The next Pokémon he wants to see is Roselia, which can be found on Route 16. You'll be awarded a Leaf Stone. The next Pokémon he wants to see is Lotad, which can be found in the Beach area of the Kanto Safari Zone. You'll be awarded a Deepseatooth. The next Pokémon he wants to see is Growlithe, which can be found on Route 36. You'll be awarded a Sun Stone. The final Pokémon he wants to see is Pichu, which can be found on Route 31. You'll be awarded a Deepseascale. That's it for here, Fly back to Cerulean and heal up, it's time to take of the Gym!

Cerulean City Gym

As you enter the gym, head towards the trainer closest to you to the east to battle Swimmer Parker, he has two Lv 57 Seadras and a Lv 57 Gyarados. The trainer on the west side of this pool is Swimmer Diana who has a Lv 57 Golduck, a Lv 57 Cloyster, and a Lv 57 Corsola. Just below Misty to the north is Swimmer Briana, she has two Lv 58 Seakings and a Lv 58 Azumarill. The final battle is with Leader Misty, she has a Lv 62 Golduck, a Lv 62 Quagsire holding a Quick Claw, a Lv 61 Vaporeon holding Leftovers, a Lv 61 Milotic, a Lv 62 Lapras holding Nevermeltice, and a Lv 63 Starmie holding Mystic Water. You'll be awarded the Cascadebadge upon victory, as well as TM03 (Water Pulse).

Route 25 Revisited/Suicune Encounter

After defeating Misty, we can finally finish up the Suicune arc! Take the bridge north of Cerulean City toward Route 24, head east towards Route 25, and make your way east through the small maze of trainers to get back to the cliff where you initially found Misty on her date. This time, you'll see Suicune on the cliff. As you approach from the south, Eusine will catch up and finally concede that you are the chosen trainer for Suicune. Interact with it to battle a Lv 50 Suicune.

From Saffron City, head out of town via the eastern gate to reach Route 8. You'll find Biker Dwayne to the east as you enter the route, he has a Lv 57 Koffing, a Lv 58 Koffing, a Lv 59 Koffing, and a Lv 60 Koffing. Head south to find Biker Harris who has a Lv 59 Flareon. Head east to battle Biker Zeke who has a Lv 58 Crobat and a Lv 56 Muk. Follow the dirt path north and then east to battle Super Nerd Sam who has a Porygon2. From here, walk south back onto the dirt path, and follow it east to reach Lavender Town.

Lavender Town Radio Tower/Radio Upgrade

After fixing the generator at the Power Plant, head to the Radio Tower just east of the Pokécenter in Lavender Town and enter. Speak to the old man NPC to be given the Radio Upgrade. With this, we can now awaken the Snorlax in Vermilion City, let's Fly there now!

Vermilion City Revisited/Snorlax

From the Pokécenter in Vermilion, follow the street east, south at the first branch, south at the second branch, west at the third branch into a wall, north up some stairs, and then east to find Snorlax. After acquiring the Radio Upgrade, press A to awaken and battle a Lv 50 Snorlax holding Leftovers. The cave opening previously blocked by Snorlax is the entrance to Diglett's Cave, our next target.

Diglett's Cave

Encounter List LandRock Smash/HeadbuttSurfOld RodGood RodSuper Rod
Diglett — 85% (Lv 2-16)
Dugtrio — 15% (Lv 16)

After entering the cave from Vermilion City, you'll descend a ladder to reach the main tunnel. The path is a straight shot northwest to a ladder which you will ascend. In the next room, take the exit to the south to reach Route 2.

Route 2
