After exiting Diglett's Cave, head south and enter the house. Speaking to the NPC inside will net you a Nugget. Exit the house, Cut the tree to the south, and walk south through a gate. Continue south to find a Carbos. Even further south is a Nugget on the ground. Make your way back north all the way to the entrance to Diglett's Cave and Cut the tree to the west. Go north from here to reach Pewter City!

Pewter City/Legendary Wing

As you enter Pewter City from Route 2 to the south, walk north to find the Pokécenter. From the Pokécenter, head east, north at the fork in the road, continuing north at the next fork. Wondering around the third fork just ahead is a chubby NPC who can teach your Pokémon Rollout. Head back south, this time heading east onto the street in front of the Mart, and then north up some stairs onto a small cliff occupied by an NPC who is an old man. Talk to him to receive either the Silver Wing or the Rainbow Wing, dependant on what legendary you chose to face earlier in Johto. If you have followed the walkthrough to this point, you will receive the Silver Wing, and the section revolviong around the Lugia encounter will be explained once we finish up in Pewter City. For those who already have Lugia, here is a link to the section of the walkthrough about the Tin Tower/Ho-oh. From the old man, head back down the stairs and to the street to the east. Head north up some stairs to find a berry tree which yields 10 Pomeg Berries.

Pewter City Museum/Claw & Root Fossil

Our next target will be the east wing of the Pewter City Museum to the north. From the Pokécenter, head east, north at the first fork, and continue north past the second and third forks as far as you can go. Head west and then north up some stairs in front of the museum. Rather than entering, head east and enter the small building you see connected to the museum. On a table you will see two fossils, interact with them to receive the Claw Fossil and the Root Fossil. The scientist at the southern desk can resurrect fossils. Exit this building and head into the main entrance of the museum. You'll see a hiker NPC on the north side of the first floor who is looking for Shards. You can trade a Red Shard for a Sun Stone, a Yellow Shard for a Deepseatooth, a Green Shard for a Leaf Stone, and a Blue Shard for a Deepseascale. That wraps up the valuables around Pewter, exit the museum, head down the stairs, and follow the street west, south, and then east to reach the entrance to Pewter City Gym.

Pewter City Gym

This gym is about as straight forward as they come! Head north to battle Camper Jerry who has a Lv 60 Dugtrio, a lv 58 Sandslash, and a Lv 60 Donphan. Just ahead is Leader Brock, he has Lv 66 Golem holding a Quick Claw, a Lv 66 Aerodactyl holding a Hard Stone, a Lv 66 Kabutops holding a Scope Lens, a Lv 66 Omastar holding a Focus Band, a Lv 66 Relicanth holding a Hard Stone, and a Lv 66 Kleavor holding a Quick Claw. You'll be awarded the Boulderbadge upon victory, as well as TM26 (Earthquake).

Seadra — 5% (Lv 40)

If you've been following the walkthrough up to this point, you've already battled Ho-oh and will be ready to take on this section once you acquire the SIlver Wing in Kanto. If instead you elected to pursue Lugia first, you can take on this section once you've acquired the Silver WIng and the Clear Bell, and have taken on the Kimono Girls after receiving your 8th badge. Ensure you are stocked up on Pokeballs (Love or Level Balls ftw), as well as some Escape Ropes, then Fly to Cianwood. From the Pokécenter, walk to the southeastern corner of the island and begin surfing southeast. Just past a Swimmer close to Cianwood, you'll see the Southwest entrance into the Whirl Islands. You'll have to surf along the southern portion of this island, before going north and traversing across a whirlpool to the west in order to reach the entrance. Inside, go north and then descend a ladder. Ascend that same ladder to return to the first floor and grab the Full Restore east of the ladder. Descend and then reascend once more to come back to the first floor, then head south, and Surf east across a small pond. You'll find a Guard Spec to the south. Head north and descend the ladder. On the next floor, take two steps east, begin surfing east until you hit a cliff, then surf north until you disembark onto land to the north. Take four steps north, ignore the stairs you see to the north, and beging walking east. After a short distance, you'll see the path branch to the south, follow it south until you find a Big Nugget. Return to the stars we previously ignored by heading north and then west. After ascending the stairs, you'll see a cave with a large opening. Before entering the cave, walk around to the backside to find a Carbos. Walk back around and enter the cave. In the next room, follow the path north and then east and descend the ladder. Head west across the next room to find another ladder which you will descend. Head east across the next room to find yet another ladder which you will descend. In the next room, walk to the northwest corner of the small island you are on and begin surfing east. Ignore the small patch of land in front of a stairset to the north, and instead disembark on land further east which has a large cave opening. Within this cave is Lugia. If you've gotten here before acquiring the Silver Wing and defeating the Kimono Girls in Ecruteak, a Tentacruel will block your path. Speaking to it three times will start an encounter with a Lv 100 Tentacruel. You cannot catch this Pokémon, and if you manage to knock it out, it will heal itself and continue to block the path into the cave until you have gotten the Silver Wing and defeated the Kimono Girls. Inside the cave, if you selected "Silver" when speaking to Baoba earlier in the game, the Kimono Girls will be here. There will be a cutscene where they will perform a dance to summon a Lv 50 Lugia. If you selected "Gold" the Lugia you find will be Lv 70. Once you've battled Lugia, we will get back to collecting the rest of the items within the Whirl Islands, so go ahead and head south to exit the cave. Back outside of Lugia's cave, walk five steps east and then surf east a short distance and disembark onto the land in front of a tall set of stairs. Ascend the stairs, head west, ascend another set of stairs, head east, and then ascend yet another set of stairs. Head east to find a PP Max. Return west and surf north, ascend a waterfall, then disembark onto land to the east to find a Full Restore. Surf west and ascend another waterfall to the north. Disembark on the land to the east and ascend the ladder you see to the north. On the next floor, follow the path to the northeast to find a ladder which you will ascend, leading to an Ultra Ball. Use an escape rope to avoid having to make your way back to the entrance, we will need to enter from one of the other islands to collect the remaining items. Fly back to Cianwood and head to the northern shore directly north of the Pokécenter. We will be aiming to enter the northwest Whirl Island. Surf north 3 spaces, then head east. Traverse the whirlpool you see to the northeast and enter the cave. Inside, head east and descend the ladder you see to the northeast. On the next floor, walk two spaces west, then head south. You'll descend the easternmost small staircase to the south, walk east three spaces, then ascend another small staircase. Start making your way south, eventually descending a ladder you find to the southeast. In this room you will find a Max Revive. Ascend the ladder to return to the previous floor. Head south from the ladder and then west to eventually find a Calcium. Make your way back east to the ladder we just descended, north, south down the small set of stairs, east, and north up another small set of stairs. Continue north towards the ladder we originally descended down to this flood, then head south down the set of stairs to the southeast of the ladder. Continue south into the cliff, then head north up the stairs just east of your position. Continue north into the cliff, then descend the stairs you see to the southeast. You'll find a Full Restore. Use an Escape Rope here, we only have two more items to find in the northeast Whirl Island! Back outside, surf towards the whirlpool you see to the east and traverse across it. Continue east to find another whirlpool which you will traverse, and enter the cave opening just northwest of the whirlpool. Inside, head east and jump the ledge and collect a Rare Candy. Descend the ladder southwest of your position. Surf west across the water to find the final item in the Whirl Islands, an Escape Rope. Go ahead and use that to return outside!

Head east out of Pewter City to reach Route 3. The first trainer you will face is Youngster Jimmy, he has a Lv 59 Raticate and a Lv 59 Arbok. Head north through a gap between ledges into a cliff and then east to battle Youngster Warren who has a Lv 60 Fearow. Continue east, south through a gap between a ledge and a cliff, and then east to battle Firebreather Otis. Otis has two Lv 58 Magmars and a Lv 60 Weezing. Continue east, head north through a gap between a ledge and a cliff, east, and jump a ledge to the south to find Firebreather Burt who has a Lv 60 Weezing and a Lv 60 Magcargo. Head east into some grass to find a berry tree which yields 10 Pomeg Berries. Head north through a gap between a ledge and a cliff to reach the western portion of Route 4.

Route 4 West

As you enter Route 4 from Route 3 to the south, walk north to find a Pokécenter. Head west from the entrance to of the Pokécenter to find an Ultra Ball. Inside the Pokécenter is move tutor who can teach your Pokémon Swagger. There is nothing else to do on this route, be sure and heal up before entering Mt. Moon to the east, you'll have a battle as soon as you enter.

After defeating Silver, our next objective is to head up to Mt. Moon Square, and area reachable by the two ladders within Mt. Moon proper itself. Note: if you've played Gen 1 Pokémon games before, you'll notice as you explore that Mt. Moon has seemingly had some cave ins. There are two ladders surrounded by rocks that are unreachable, and a certain scientist has found himself in a rocky situation. The quickest way to reach Mt. Moon Square from the entrance is to head east into a cliff, three steps south, east past a small sign to the north, and then north at the first branch in the path. Ascend the ladder ahead to reach the Square.

Mt. Moon Square/Jirachi

After entering Mt. Moon Square, head east to find a house. Inside is a vendor who sells Moon Stones. The other NPC tells you that at night time, Clefairy come out to play, clueing you in to your next move. If it isn't already night time, adjust the time using the clock inside this house. Exit, jump the ledge to the south, take three steps south, and the head east into a rock. You'll find a Moon Stone to the south. From here, head north through a gap in the fence. Assuming it is night time, you will see a group of Clefairies playing around a small pond. Make your way north to find an unusual white rock. Select "Yes" when prompted to touch the rock which causes Jirachi to appear! Interact with it start the encounter, it will be Lv 50. Once you finish the enocunter with Jirachi, it will be time to make our way to the exit of Mt. Moon. Make your way out of the fenced area, and head east until you run into a cliff. You'll enter the mouth in the cave you see to the southeast, which takes you back into Mt. Moon proper. From the ladder you just descended, head east, north up some steps, east into a cliff, south into another cliff, east into a third cliff, and then south into the wall below. You'll ascend the staircase you see to the east which leads to the exit out of Mt. Moon!

As you enter Route 4 from Mt. Moon to the west, head east to see a berry tree which yields 10 Tamato Berries. Next to this tree is a move tutor who can teach your Pokémon Dynamicpunch. Head north through a gap between ledges, east, north through a gap between a ledge and some trees, and north through one more gap between ledges to battle Picnicker Hope who has a Lv 61 Ampharos. You'll find an Hp Up to the east between two ledges. That completes Route 4!

Viridian Forest

From Pewter City, head south out of town towards Route 2. Before heading through the gate to the south, Cut the tree you see blocking the path between two fences, and head into Diglett's Cave to the east. Assuming you've beaten him, you'll find Leader Brock inside. Talk to him to send him to the Fighting Dojo in Saffron for a rematch! Go back through the fence to Route 2 and then walk through the gate to the south. You'll find yourself in Viridian Forest. Head south to find Bug Catcher Doug who has a Lv 62 Ariados. Continue south into the treeline and the head east to find an Elixer. Head back to the dirt path to the west and go north through some grass and up some stairs to battle Bug Catcher Ed, he has three Lv 60 Beedrills. Go east, south down some stairs, continue south through the grass, and then head east once you reach the dirt path. At the end of this path, head south into some grass until you reach the cliff's edge, and the go east to find Leader Bugsy. Speak to him to transport him to the Fighting Dojo in Saffron where you can challenge him to a rematch. Just west of where Bugsy was is a Full Restore. Head back east and continue north until you ascend some stairs to find a Revive. Head north until you hit the treeline, go east, and then south down some stairs and into the treeline. Go west until you reach the dirt path, follow that path south down some stairs, and go east to find a Dire Hit. Head back to the dirt path to the west, south through some grass until you hit the treeline, and then west until you find a Full Heal in a patch of grass in the southwest corner of the forest. Go east out of this patch of grass, north at the first fork until you hit a cliff, and then west to find a Max Ether. Take one step south before heading east to find a Max Potion. Head south from here, around a large tree, and take the exit out of Viridian Forest!

After exiting the gate from Viridian Forest to the north, head south through a small portion of Route 2 to reach Viridian City! The Pokécenter is in the southern portion of the town. From the entrance of the Pokécenter, head west and Surf across a small pond to find a move tutor who can teach your Pokémon Sleep Talk. Just northeast of the Pokécenter is the Mart. Inside of the mart is a move tutor who can teach your Pokémon Double-Edge. The gym leader is currently gone from his gym, we won't be able to challenge him until we get our 15th badge.

Contest Hall

The building directly north of the Viridian City Pokécenter is the Contest Hall. I will update this section once I've had the chance to reacclimate myself with Contest mechanics, for now, I would suggest Googling "gen 3 Pokémon contests mechanics" if you're wanting to take them on.

From Pallet Town, start surfing south to enter Route 21. As you surf south, you'll see a lone NPC on an island to the east. This is Fisherman Arnold, he has a Lv 61 Lanturn and a Lv 61 Quagsire. Welcome to Cinnabar Island! There isn't a whole lot left on the island itself due to a volcanic explosion. Inside the Pokécenter, the NPC with the blonde hair is a move tutor who can teach your Pokémon Explosion. Outside, you can find Blue, the gym leader of Viridian City, hanging out in front of a pond to the north of the Pokécenter. To get him to return to Viridian City for a gym battle, we must collect 15 badges and then talk to him here. Where is the Cinnabar Island Gym you might ask? Let's heal up at the Pokécenter, and continue our expedition east.

From the Pokécenter on Cinnabar Island, walk to the shoreline to the south and surf east as far as you can, north as far as you can, and then continue east to reach Route 20. Just as you enter the route, you'll see a Fisherman and his golden Magikarp named Dennis. Best not to antagonize that particular fish, I've seen it Splash legendaries to death firsthand! Cinnabar Gym (Seafoam Islands)

As you enter the western entrance to the Seafoam Islands from Route 20, descend the ladder you see to the north to find the hidden Cinnabar Gym. The only trainer here is Leader Blaine. Blaine has a Lv 66 Rapidash holding a Quick Claw, a Lv 65 Magmortar holding a Sitrus Berry, a Lv 66 Houndoom holding a Miracle Seed, a Lv 65 Magcargo holding Leftovers, a Lv 67 Torkoal holding a White Herb, and a Lv 67 Camerupt holding Charcoal. Victory will net you the Volcanobadge and TM50 (Overheat).

Seafoam Islands/Articuno

As you enter the southern entrance to the Seafoam Islands from Route 20 to the west, walk west, south down a large set of stairs, south down a small set of stairs, seven steps west, and thirteen steps north. We have to bike across some broken ice here, so mount your bike and press R to switch gears.

Cross the three cracked ice tiles to the west and stop. From the now exposed hole in the ice, take three steps west to align yourself with a cracked ice tile to the north. Mount your bike, press R to shift gears, and bike north across the ice, some solid ground, and then eventually into some rocks above two rows of cracked ice, which you will fall through.

Falling through the ice will land you in some water. Surf north to find Articuno; it will be Lv 50. To exit the cave from here, ascend the stairs west of Articuno. Jump the ledge south of the ladder to skate across a large patch of ice. Continue south into a rock, then take a step east to fall through cracked ice. Walk south, surf south across some water onto land, take four steps south, walk east until you reach some water, and surf south until you disembark onto land. Walk west, north up some stairs, and ascend the ladder. Walk south down a small set of stairs, east, north up a small set of stairs, and then north up a large set of stairs. The exit is to the east of the top of the large set of stairs!

Cinnabar Island Revisited/Blue

Once you have 15 badges, Fly to Cinnabar and speak to the NPC standing in the center of the island. This is the Viridian City gym leader, Blue, and he will head back to Viridian where we can now challenge him and get our final badge.

From the Fuchsia City Pokécenter, head south out of town to Route 19 and begin surfing south. Shortly after entering the water, you will battle Swimmer Jerome. He has a Lv 58 Vaporeon, a Lv 58 Tentacruel, and a Lv 58 Seaking. Surf 5-6 spaces west before continuing to surf south. You'll see two NPCs side by side in the water. The NPC on the left is Swimmer Tucker who has a Lv 60 Quilfish and a Lv 60 Cloyster. The NPC on the right is Swimmer Dawn who has a Lv 60 Seaking. Surf 8-9 spaces east before continuing south to battle Swimmer Harold, he has a Lv 62 Octillery and a Lv 61 Seadra. Below Harold is a cave that will be blocked off until you beat the Kanto League. Surf west from the cave to reach the eastern portion of Route 20. Continue surfing west on Route 20 to find Swimmer Cameron on the southern portion of this route. Cameron has a Lv 64 Azumarill. Continue surfing west and then north to battle Swimmer Nicole who has a Lv 60 Mantine, and a Lv 63 Lapras. Disembark onto the shore and continue west, you'll find a Shell Bell on the beach close to the northern entrance to Seafoam Islands. That is it for these routes, Fly to Viridian City and heal up, it's time to get our final badge!

Viridian City Gym

From the Pokécenter in Viridian, head to the gym in the northeast portion of town. Assuming you have collected 15 badges and spoken to Leader Blue on Cinnabar, he will be awaiting you as the sole trainer in his gym. Blue has a Lv 69 Rhyperior holding a Sitrus Berry, a Lv 68 Pidgeot holding a Sharp Beak, a Lv 67 Machamp holding a Scope Lens, a Lv 68 Exeggutor holding Leftovers, a Lv 68 Tyranitar holding a Focus Band, and a Lv 69 Arcanine holding a Silk Scarf. Upon victory you'll be awarded the Earthbadge and TM08 (Bulk Up).

Pallet Town/Oak Unlocking Mt. Silver

After acquiring your sixteenth badge, Fly to Pallet Town and head into Oak's Laboratory. Speaking to Oak will result in him lauding you for collecting all the badges in Kanto and will unlock Mt. Silver. We won't head there quite yet, but it is reachable via the gate leading to Victory Road.

Route 22/Celebi Cutscene

Route 22 is a small route west of Viridian which leads to the gate connecting Johto to Kanto right before Victory Road. There is an optional cutscene available to those who have caught Celebi, if you have, make sure Celebi is at the head of your party before heading to Route 22. From the Viridian City Pokécenter, head west, north at the bend in the road, west at the first branch in the road, and then continue west to reach Route 22. Jump the ledge to the south and follow the brick road west. As you pass the fourth streetlamp, Celebi will suddenly jump and transport you back in time where you will discover the parentage and true motive of your Rival. There is one more event unlocked by Celebi, so let's check that out before taking on the Kanto League!

Tohjo Falls/Celebi Cutscene/Giovanni Battle

Be sure Celebi is in your party and heal your party, we have a battle coming up. Remember that old radio in Tohjo Falls? It's time to head back and find out what that was. Fly to New Bark Town and Surf east out of town onto Route 27 and enter the cave opening into Tohjo Falls. Climb the waterfall, surf east, descend another waterfall, and then disembark onto the land to the west. After walking west a short distance, you'll see a cave opening to the north. Put Celebi at the head of your party and enter the cave. After taking a step inside, Celebi will jump and transport you back in time where you will witness Giovanni hearing the Rocket broadcast from the radio tower alerting him to their return to power. He will inform you of his intent to rejoin his team at Goldenrod and will challenge you to a battle. Rocket Admin Giovanni has a Lv 60 Kangaskhan holding Leftovers, a Lv 61 Persian, a Lv 61 Honchkrow, a Lv 61 Nidoqueen, a Lv 60 Ursaluna, and a Lv 62 Nidoking. Getting beat by a child once more causes Giovanni to ditch his dreams for good!

Assuming you have acquired all 16 badges, it is now time to take on the Kanto Elite 4! Fly to Indigo Plateau and heal up. You'll see your Rival in front of the entrance leading to the Elite 4, he will challenge you one final time. His potential parties are:

Upon victory, your party will be once again entered into the Hall Of Fame. You'll in New Bark Town after the credits roll. Any legendaries and static encounters (like Sudowoodo) will now respawn, and will continue to respawn upon each subsequent defeat of the Kanto E4 going forward. The GS Ball will also respawn at it's location in the Ruins of Alph, it has the second best catch odds of any ball in game, so be sure and snag that!

Kanto Safari Zone Steven/Eon Ticket

After you've beaten the Kanto E4, Fly to Fuchsia and head to the Safari Zone to the north. Speak to Steven (the NPC with gray hair at a table) to receive the Eon Ticket! We will be using this shortly.

South Olivine City/Auroraticket

Fly to Olivine City. From the Pokécenter, walk south as far as you can, and then walk eleven steps east. Assuming you've beaten the Kanto E4, talking to the NPC standing under the canopy will net you the Auroraticket! We will be using this shortly.

Southern Island/Latias & Latios

Once you have acquired the Eon Ticket from Steven, Fly to Olivine. From the Pokécenter, head south into the pier where the SS Aqua is docked. Speak to the Sailor and select Southern Island. There is only one path, follow it to enter a forest area to the north. Here you will find two Pokémon. The red one is a Lv 50 Latias, the blue is a Lv 50 Latios. After battling both, return to the Sailor and speak to him to head back to Olivine.

Birth Island/Deoxys

Once you have acquired the Auroraticket from the NPC in Olivine, head south into the pier where the SS Aqua is docked. Speak to the Sailor and select Birth Island. This is a small island featuring four meteorites scattered around, and a triangle in the center. Interacting with the triangle will cause it to move. To summon the legendary here, you must take the shortest path to the triangle and interact with it after it moves. To start, interact with the southern face. Take one step south, three steps west, then interact with the eastern face. Take three steps east, five steps north, then interact with the southern face. Take five steps south, three steps east, then interact with the western face. Take three steps north, five steps west, then interact with the eastern face. Take four steps east then interact with the western face. Take two steps west, two steps south, then interact with the northern face. Take one step south, three steps west, then interact with the eastern face. Take six steps east and interact with the western face. Take three steps west and interact with the northern face. Finally, take three steps north then interact with the southern face to summon a Lv 70 Deoxys. Note: Unlike most stationary Pokémon, Deoxys will flee if you run away, so be sure and save before interacting with the triangle the final time if you'd like to shiny hunt Deoxys. Soft resetting is necessary to accomplish this feat. If you have Deoxys in your party and return to Birth Island, you can interact with the meteorites to change it's form. Each form affects the stats and the Ability of Deoxys. The northern meteorite draws out the Speed Form, the western meteorite draws out the Attack Form, the meteorite in the eastern corner draws out the Normal Form, and the meteorite just north of the one in the eastern corner draws out the Defense Form. You can change Deoxys' form as often as you'd like!

Route 47 Revisited/Embedded Tower/Rayquaza

Once you've beaten the Kanto League, Rayquaza is now available to catch. Fly to the Safari Zone Gate. From there, head south across a bridge, east across a bridge, south past a berry tree and down a small set of stairs, west across a bridge, south, east across a bridge, north, east across a bridge, and north into a cave. Descend the ladder, descend another ladder, then south out of the cave. Surf south as far as you can, head west past a beach occupied by a Staryu, then disembark on a beach with a tall staircase. Head north up two staircases then enter the cave entrance into the Embedded Tower. Inside you can encounter a Lv 70 Rayquaza.

Route 19 Revisited/Kyogre

Once you've beaten the Kanto League, Kyogre is now available to catch. Fly to Fuchsia City and head south out of town towards Route 19. Surf south as far as you can and disembark on the bank of a cave on the southeast corner of the route. Inside this cave is a Lv 70 Kyogre.

Seafoam Islands Revisited/Groudon

Once you've beaten the Kanto League, Groudon is now available to catch. Fly to Cinnabar Island and surf east towards Route 20. Continue surfing east until you reach the southern entrance to the Seafoam Islands. Enter the cave and descend the ladder into Blaine's Gym. Behind the platform where Blaine previously stood is a cave entrance. In here, you'll find a Lv 70 Groudon.

Cerulean Cave/Mewtwo

Once you have beaten the Kanto League, Cerulean Cave will be open. Fly to Cerulean and head north out of town towards Route 24. After crossing the bridge, surf south on the water west of the bridge until you can disembark on land in front of a cave opening. This is the entrance to Cerulean Cave. After entering the cave, smash a rock and surf north, disembarking on the land just north of the entrance with a ladder you can ascend. Ascend the ladder and follow the path, smashing two rocks along the way, to find a Protein. Return to and descend the ladder back to the first floor and begin surfing west. Once you get to a branch in the path, disembark onto the land to the north. The ladder you see to the north leads nowhere, so head west and collect the PP Up on the ground. Head south from where you just found the PP Up and ascend the ladder you see just north of a smashable rock. Once again, follow the path, smashing one rock along the way, to find a Zinc. Return to and descend the ladder back to the first floor. Head south from the ladder, smash the rock, and collect the Rare Candy on the ground. Smash another rock in front of a small staircase to the east, head up the stairs onto a cliff, and head east as far as you can. Head north to find a Max Elixer just east of a ladder. Ascend that ladder and follow the short path south, smashing a rock along the way, to find a Big Pearl. Return to and descend the ladder back to the first floor. Head west, smash a rock in front of a small set of stairs to the south, and ascend the ladder you see on a small cliff to the east. This path isn't as straightforward as the others we have encountered in this cave to this point. Head west, then turn south at the first branch in the path. Proceed west, then south two steps, east four steps, and follow this path south, east, north, and then east until you reach a ladder, which you will descend. Descend one more ladder on the next floor then head north, east, then smash a rock in front of a small set of stairs. Head east, north, then east and smash a rock. Hugging the wall to the north, proceed east until you can go north between two cliffs to find an Ultra Ball. Walk south as far as you can, then west and smash a rock in front of a small set of stairs. Ascend the small set of stairs, take two steps east, then head north to find a Max Revive. Head west and smash a rock in front a small set of stairs to the south. Follow this path south until you come across another rock blocking a set of stairs, smash that rock and ascend the stairs. Surf ten spaces west on the water you see to the west, surf north, and disembark onto a small cliff where you can encounter a Lv 80 Mewtwo!

Gym Leader Rematches Summary

As previously discussed, you can find the gym leaders at various places in the overworld after defeating them. Speaking to them will cause them to transport to the Fighting Dojo in Saffron City where you can challenge them to rematches which net 10 BP upon defeat. Most of them can be found as soon as you defeat the Johto League, though there are a couple of exceptions that will be noted below. If you've followed the walkthrough to this point, there are a handful that we have already encountered. This section will consolidate that information so one can use this section to find everyone.

Falkner: Falkner is found on the fifth floor of the Celadon Department Store.

Bugsy: Bugsy is found in Viridian Forest.

Whitney: Whitney is found on the sixth floor of the Goldenrod Department Store.

Morty: Morty is found in front of this house on the southeast corner of Bellchime Trail which leads to the Tin Tower.

Chuck: Chuck is apparently still "training" in his gym.

Jasmine: Jasmine is found inside the Olivine Cafe, the building just west of the Pokécenter.

Pryce: Pryce is found on the southwest side of the Lake of Rage.

Claire: Claire is found in the Dragon's Den.

Brock: Brock is found inside the entrance to Diglett's Cave, accessible from Route 2.

Misty: Misty is found just south of the Sea Cottage on Route 25.

LT Surge: LT Surge is found in front of the Power Plant.

Erika: Erika is found just south of the Celadon Department Store.

Janine: Janine is found in the gate leading to Victory Road. The quickest way to get here is through Route 22.

Sabrina: Sabrina is found on the Olivine Docks.

Blaine: Blaine is found on Cinnabar Island after acquiring your sixteenth badge.

Blue: Blue is found in his gym after acquiring his badge. Exit and reenter the gym after defeating him to trigger the new dialogue.

Here are the teams used by the Gym Leaders for their rematches! The Johto leaders will have new teams, the Kanto ones will retain the teams they originally had. You can challenge them however many times you'd like.

Once you've acquired all sixteen badges and then spoken to Oak, the path to Mt. Silver will be unlocked. Head west out of Viridian City to Route 22. On the western side of this route is the gate that leads to Victory Road. As you enter the gate, walk across the center hallway to the hallway going west and exit. You'll now be on Route 28! Take one step west, then walk south into a cliff. Take twelve steps west, surf across the small pond into grass to the south, walk west until you hit a tree, one step north, west into a cliff, south, and then north up a small staircase. Walk west until you see another staircase to the north, head north up the stairs, and then west until you see yet another staircase. Walk north up these stairs, and follow the dirt path north and then west until you come to a Pokécenter. From the entrance of the Pokécenter, walk east into the grass next to the Pokécenter, north into a cliff, then east until you see a staircase. Head south down these stairs, east, and south down another set of stairs. Head east on the path while hugging the trees to the north until you hit a cliff, north, until you see a set of stairs, then head south down these stairs. Walk east and enter the house you see to the north. Speak to the NPC in this house to receive TM47 (Steel Wing). From here, either walk back or Fly to the Pokécenter outside of Mt. Silver.

From the entrance of the Pokécenter, jump the ledge to the east and head north to find a cave opening into Mt. Silver. After entering the mountain, walk west and begin surfing. Surf two spaces west and then north up a waterfall. Dismbark onto a staircase to the north and enter the cave opening you see. Inside this cave is a Spell Tag. Exit the cave, surf west, and disembark on the bank to the west. Head up the small staircase and walk west to find an Ultra Ball. Return to the water and surf north, disembarking onto the tall staircase you see. You'll find a Full Restore on the ground to the west. Head back east to the small staircase slightly north, surf north up a waterfall, disembark onto a staircase to the north, then enter the cave opening you see. Walk west into a cliff, north, east at the first branch in the path, and then south down a large set of stairs. You'll encounter a Lv 50 Moltres here. After your encounter with Moltres, either walk back to the entrance of Mt Silver, or use an Escape Rope.

Back at the entrance of Mt. Silver, walk north up a large staircase to find a Max Potion. Walk through the cave opening you see to the east. Outside, walk east into the cliffside, south, north up a set of stairs, west, north up another set of stairs, west, and north up yet another set of stairs. Continue north, south down a set of stairs to the east, six steps south, and enter the cave opening to the west. Inside this cave you will find a Dire Hit. Exit the cave and go south down a set of stairs to the east. Walk east, south down a set of stairs, and make your way to another set of stairs to the south. Go north up these stairs and walk west into a cave opening to the west. Once inside, walk west until you can head north, continuing north up a large set of stairs and into a cave opening to the north. In the next room, walk north up a set of stairs, west into a cliff, north up another set of stairs, west into the wall, south until you hit another wall, east, north at the branch in the path and up a set of stairs to the north. Walk west into a cliff, north up a set of stairs, and then east until you see a cave opening to the north. You'll arrive back outside in a snowy portion of Mt. Silver. Walk east as far as you can and then south, ignoring the first cave opening you see to the west. Continue south and enter the second cave opening you see to the west. Inside, walk south and exit a cave opening you see to the east. Back outside, walk south as far as you can, west past a cave opening to the north, north up a set of stairs you see just west of the cave opening, west, north up a second set of stairs, east, and then north to find a Nevermeltice. Head back south down the two sets of stairs we just climbed and then enter the cave opening just east of the second set of stairs. Head west and then south through a cave opening to the south. Back outside, head north up a set of stairs to the east of the cave opening, then west. Head north up the westernmost staircase, continuing north up a second staircase to find a Rare Candy. Head back south down the two sets of stairs, and then head north up the set of stairs just east of the second set of stairs. Continue north up another set of stairs and enter the cave opening to the north. Inside, head west, north up a large set of stairs, and enter the cave opening to the north. On the summit, you will find PKMN Trainer Red to the north. Prepare yourself for the most difficult battle in the main storyline of the game! Red's team is:

After defeating Red, the credits will roll, and you will reawaken in New Bark Town. Congratulations, assuming you've followed the walkthrough, you've beaten the main storyline of the game! If you haven't completed your Pokedex, now would be a great time to do so. Remember, once you've completed your dex, you can talk to the Game Designer inside Celadon Mansion to receive your Diploma. After completing your dex, the Battle Frontier will likely hold the final content for you to exhaust! I'd recommend starting a new run and playing around with the randomizer settings for a completely unique run, even after several playthroughs during my playtesting, the randomizer was a gamechanger for this Pokémon Trainer. There are currently a bunch of ways for you to change up the core experience. Also, stay tuned, our wonderful devs have even more new features in store for the eventual release of V2.0.
